{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01SqkuE6i5Ec6c37zLEcEM4N","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01S6EKfiQTK8CwPKiM737GzR","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/text-normalization-categories.md","content":"# Text Normalization for Product Categories\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers techniques for normalizing and standardizing product category names across different e-commerce platforms.\n\n## Core Normalization Functions\n\n```python\nimport re\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef normalize_category_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Standardize category text for consistency.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return ''\n\n    text = str(text).strip()\n\n    # Convert to title case\n    text = text.title()\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace\n    text = ' '.join(text.split())\n\n    # Standardize common variations\n    replacements = {\n        ' And ': ' & ',\n        ' Or ': ' | ',\n        \"'S \": \"'s \",\n        ' - ': ' | ',\n        ', ': ' | ',\n    }\n    for old, new in replacements.items():\n        text = text.replace(old, new)\n\n    return text\n\ndef remove_redundant_words(child, parent):\n    \"\"\"Remove parent category words from child to avoid overlap.\"\"\"\n    parent_words = set(parent.lower().split())\n    child_words = child.split()\n    filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n    return ' '.join(filtered) if filtered else child\n```\n\n## Word Overlap Calculation\n\n```python\ndef calculate_word_overlap(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate percentage of word overlap between two texts.\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    min_size = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return len(intersection) / min_size if min_size > 0 else 0.0\n\ndef check_sibling_overlap(categories):\n    \"\"\"Check if sibling categories have <30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            overlap = calculate_word_overlap(cat1, cat2)\n            if overlap > 0.30:\n                print(f\"High overlap: {cat1} vs {cat2} = {overlap:.2%}\")\n```\n\n## Creating Representative Names\n\n```python\ndef create_representative_name(subcategories, max_words=5, separator=' | '):\n    \"\"\"Create a name that represents 70%+ of subcategories.\"\"\"\n    # Count word frequencies across all subcategories\n    word_freq = Counter()\n    for subcat in subcategories:\n        words = subcat.lower().split()\n        word_freq.update(words)\n\n    # Get most common words\n    total_cats = len(subcategories)\n    representative_words = []\n\n    for word, count in word_freq.most_common():\n        if count / total_cats >= 0.70:  # 70%+ representation\n            representative_words.append(word.title())\n        if len(representative_words) >= max_words:\n            break\n\n    # If no words meet 70% threshold, use most common\n    if not representative_words:\n        representative_words = [word.title() for word, _ in word_freq.most_common(min(3, max_words))]\n\n    return separator.join(representative_words)\n```\n\n## Standardization Patterns\n\n```python\n# Common category name patterns\nSTANDARD_MAPPINGS = {\n    # Convert to title case\n    text = text.title()\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace\n    text = ' '.join(text.split())\n\n    # Standardize common variations\n    replacements = {\n        ' And ': ' & ',\n        ' Or ': ' | ',\n        \"'S \": \"'s \",\n        ' - ': ' | ',\n        ', ': ' | ',\n    }\n    for old, new in replacements.items():\n        text = text.replace(old, new)\n\n    return text\n\ndef remove_redundant_words(child, parent):\n    \"\"\"Remove parent category words from child to avoid overlap.\"\"\"\n    parent_words = set(parent.lower().split())\n    child_words = child.split()\n    filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n    return ' '.join(filtered) if filtered else child\n```\n\n## Word Overlap Calculation\n\n```python\ndef calculate_word_overlap(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate percentage of word overlap between two texts.\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    min_size = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return len(intersection) / min_size if min_size > 0 else 0.0\n\ndef check_sibling_overlap(categories):\n    \"\"\"Check if sibling categories have <30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(categories):\n        for cat2 in categories[i+1:]:\n            overlap = calculate_word_overlap(cat1, cat2)\n            if overlap > 0.30:\n                print(f\"High overlap: {cat1} vs {cat2} = {overlap:.2%}\")\n```\n\n## Creating Representative Names\n\n```python\ndef create_representative_name(subcategories, max_words=5, separator=' | '):\n    \"\"\"Create a name that represents 70%+ of subcategories.\"\"\"\n    # Normalize to exactly 5 levels:\n\n```python\ndef normalize_to_5_levels(levels):\n    \"\"\"Ensure exactly 5 levels, padding or truncating as needed.\"\"\"\n    result = levels[:5]  # Truncate if more than 5\n\n    # Pad with empty strings if less than 5\n    while len(result) < 5:\n        result.append('')\n\n    return result\n```\n\n## Avoiding Parent-Child Name Overlap\n\n```python\ndef remove_parent_overlap(child_name, parent_name):\n    \"\"\"Remove words from child that appear in parent.\"\"\"\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', '&', '|', 'for', 'of', 'in', 'with'}\n\n    parent_words = set(w.lower() for w in parent_name.split() if w.lower() not in stop_words)\n    child_words = child_name.split()\n\n    filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n\n    if not filtered:\n        # If all words removed, keep original\n        return child_name\n\n    return ' '.join(filtered)\n```\n\n## Sibling Distinctiveness Check\n\n```python\ndef ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(siblings, max_overlap=0.30):\n    \"\"\"Ensure siblings have <30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n    conflicts = []\n\n    for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n        for j, sib2 in enumerate(siblings[i+1:], i+1):\n            words1 = set(sib1.lower().split())\n            words2 = set(sib2.lower().split())\n\n            if not words1 or not words2:\n                continue\n\n            overlap = len(words1 & words2) / min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n            if overlap > max_overlap:\n                conflicts.append((sib1, sib2, overlap))\n\n    return conflicts\n\ndef resolve_sibling_conflicts(siblings, conflicts):\n    \"\"\"Resolve high-overlap siblings by renaming.\"\"\"\n    renamed = {}\n\n    for sib1, sib2, _ in conflicts:\n        # Add distinguishing prefix/suffix\n        if sib1 not in renamed:\n            renamed[sib1] = sib1\n        if sib2 not in renamed:\n            # Find unique word in sib2\n            words1 = set(sib1.lower().split())\n            words2 = sib2.split()\n            unique = [w for w in words2 if w.lower() not in words1]\n            if unique:\n                renamed[sib2] = ' | '.join(unique[:3])\n            else:\n                renamed[sib2] = sib2 + ' (Alt)'\n\n    return renamed\n```\n\n## Representative Naming (70%+ Rule)\n\n```python\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef create_category_name(subcategories, max_words=5, threshold=0.70):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a category name representative of 70%+ of subcategories.\n    Uses \" | \" as separator between words.\n    \"\"\"\n    word_counter = Counter()\n    total = len(subcategories)\n\n    for subcat in subcategories:\n        words = [w.strip() for w in subcat.split() if len(w.strip()) > 2]\n        word_counter.update(words)\n\n    # Get words appearing in 70%+ of subcategories\n    representative = []\n    for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n        if count / total >= threshold:\n            representative.append(word.title())\n            if len(representative) >= max_words:\n                break\n\n    # Fallback: use top 3 most common if no words meet threshold\n    if not representative:\n        representative = [w.title() for w, _ in word_counter.most_common(3)]\n\n    return ' | '.join(representative[:max_words])\n```\n\n## Pyramid Structure Balance\n\n```python\ndef check_pyramid_balance(taxonomy):\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify taxonomy forms a reasonable pyramid:\n    - Level 1: 10-20 categories\n    - Each deeper level: 3-20 subcategories per parent\n    \"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    def check_node(node, path=''):\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if node.level == 0:  # Root\n            if not (10 <= num_children <= 20):\n                issues.append(f\"Top level has {num_children} categories (should be 10-20)\")\n        elif node.level < 5:  # Non-leaf levels\n            if num_children > 0 and not (3 <= num_children <= 20):\n                if num_children < 3:\n                    issues.append(f\"{path}/{node.name}: only {num_children} children (min 3)\")\n                elif num_children > 20:\n                    issues.append(f\"{path}/{node.name}: {num_children} children (max 20)\")\n\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            check_node(child, f\"{path}/{node.name}\")\n\n    check_node(taxonomy.root)\n    return issues\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1859,"cache_read_input_tokens":26235,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1859,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bd647b7-686e-410b-8ff1-498f3b4b66ac","uuid":"3c873041-6b60-4355-b2a0-27ce502345c1"}

\"\"\"Parse a category path string into a list of levels.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(path):\n        return []\n    path = str(path).strip()\n    # Handle both \" > \" and \">\" separators\n    if ' > ' in ============================================================================\n\nclass TaxonomyNode:\n    \"\"\"Represents a node in the taxonomy hierarchy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, name, \"\"\"Add or get a child node.\"\"\"\n        if name not in self.children:\n            self.children[name] = TaxonomyNode(name, self.level + 1)\n        return self.children[name]\n\n    def ============================================================================\n# UNIFIED TAXONOMY BUILDER\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder:\n    \"\"\"Builds and manages the unified taxonomy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = TaxonomyNode('ROOT', 0)\n        self.all_records = []\n\n    def add_category(self, source, original_path, levels):\n        \"\"\"Add a category path to the taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if not levels:\n            return\n\n        # Map first level to standardized    unified_levels.append(normalized)\n\n        # Pad to 5 levels\n        while len(unified_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n            unified_levels.append('')\n\n        # Add to tree\n        current = self.root\n        for i, level_name in enumerate(unified_levels):\n            if not level_name:\n                break\n            current = current.add_child(level_name)\n for l in levels if l]), MAX_LEVELS),\n            'unified_levels': unified_levels\n        })\n\n    def _remove_parent_overlap(self, child_name, parent_name):\n        \"\"\"Remove words from child that appear in parent.\"\"\"\n        parent_words = get_word_set(parent_name)\n        child_words = child_name.split()\n        filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n        if not filtered:\n            return child_name\n        return ' '.join(filtered)\n\n    def balance_taxonomy(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance the taxonomy to meet hierarchy constraints.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Balancing taxonomy...\")\n\n        # First, consolidate top-level categories to meet 10-20 constraint\n        self._consolidate_top_level()\n\n        # Then balance each subtree\n        self._balance_subtree(self.root)\n\n        # Update records with new unified levels\n        self._update_records()\n\n    def _consolidate_top_level(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure top level has 10-20 categories.\"\"\"\n        top_categories = list(self.root.children.keys())\n        print(f\"Initial top-level categories: {len(top_categories)}\")\n\n        if len(top_categories) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n            # Merge small categories into larger ones\n            sizes = {k: len(v.get_all_descendants()) + 1\n                    for k, v in self.root.children.items()}\n            sorted_cats = sorted(sizes.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n            while len(self.root.children) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n                # Find smallest category and merge with most similar\n                smallest = sorted_cats[0][0]\n                if smallest not in self.root.children:\n                    sorted_cats.pop(0)\n                    continue\n\n                # Find best merge target\n                best_target = None\n                best_similarity = -1\n                for other in self.root.children:\n                    if other != smallest:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(smallest, other)\n                        if sim > best_similarity:\n                            best_similarity = sim\n                            best_target = other\n\n                if best_target:\n                    # Merge smallest into best_target\n                    self._merge_nodes(self.root, smallest, best_target)\n                    sorted_cats.pop(0)\n\n        print(f\"Final top-level categories: {len(self.root.children)}\")\n\n    def _merge_nodes(self, parent, source_name, target_name):\n        \"\"\"Merge source node into target node.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in parent.children or target_name not in parent.children:\n            return\n\n        source = parent.children[source_name]\n        target = parent.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge children\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                # Recursively merge\n                self._merge_child_trees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                child_node.level = target.level + 1\n\n        # Merge sources and paths\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        target.original_paths.extend(source.original_paths)\n\n        # Remove source\n        del parent.children[source_name]\n\n    def _merge_child_trees(self, target, source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source tree into target tree.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        target.original_paths.extend(source.original_paths)\n\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_child_trees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _balance_subtree(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Balance a subtree to meet constraints.\"\"\"\n        if not node.children:\n            return\n\n        # If too many children, cluster them\n        if len(node.children) > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and node.level < MAX_LEVELS - 1:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # If too few children (and not at max level), may need to promote grandchildren\n        # But we keep it simple for now\n\n        # Recurse\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_subtree(child)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children to reduce count.\"\"\"\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n\n        # Group by first word similarity\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            first_word = name.split()[0] if name else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping helps, restructure\n        if len(groups) < len(children) and len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            new_children = {}\n            for group_name, members in groups.items():\n                if len(members) == 1:\n                    # Single member, keep as is\n                    new_children[members[0][0]] = members[0][1]\n                else:\n                    # Create intermediate node\n                    intermediate_name = self._create_representative_name(\n                        [m[0] for m in members])\n                    intermediate = TaxonomyNode(intermediate_name, parent.level + 1)\n                    for name, node in members:\n                        node.level = intermediate.level + 1\n                        intermediate.children[name] = node\n                        intermediate.sources.update(node.sources)\n                    new_children[intermediate_name] = intermediate\n\n            parent.children = new_children\n\n    def _create_representative_name(self, names, max_words=MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME):\n        \"\"\"Create a representative name for a group of categories.\"\"\"\n        word_counter = Counter()\n        total = len(names)\n\n        for name in names:\n            words = get_word_set(name)\n            word_counter.update(words)\n\n        # Get words appearing in 70%+ of names\n        representative = []\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n                if len(representative) >= max_words:\n                    break\n\n        # Fallback\n        if not representative:\n            representative = [w.title() for w, _ in word_counter.most_common(min(3, max_words))]\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:max_words]) if representative else names[0]\n\n    def _update_records(self):\n        \"\"\"Update all records with current taxonomy structure.\"\"\"\n        # Build path lookup\n        path_map = {}\n        self._build_path_map(self.root, [], path_map)\n\n        # Update each record\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            # Find the path in current taxonomy\n            current = self.root\n            new_levels = []\n\n            for level in record['unified_levels']:\n                if not level or level not in current.children:\n                    break\n                current = current.children[level]\n                new_levels.append(level)\n\n            # If path was modified, try to find closest match\n            if not new_levels:\n                # Find by source path similarity\n                best_match = self._find_best_match(record['unified_levels'])\n                if best_match:\n                    new_levels = best_match\n\n            # Pad to 5 levels\n            while len(new_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n                new_levels.append('')\n\n            record['unified_levels'] = new_levels\n\n    def _build_path_map(self, node, path, result):\n        \"\"\"Build a map of all paths in taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if node.level > 0:\n            result[tuple(path)] = node\n        for name, child in node.children.items():\n            self._build_path_map(child, path + [name], result)\n\n    def _find_best_match(self, original_levels):\n        \"\"\"Find best matching path in current taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if not original_levels or not original_levels[0]:\n            return None\n\n        # Start from top level\n        for top_name in self.root.children:\n            if calculate_word_overlap(original_levels[0], top_name) > 0.5:\n                path = [top_name]\n                current = self.root.children[top_name]\n\n                for i, level in enumerate(original_levels[1:], 1):\n                    if not level:\n                        break\n                    best_child = None\n                    best_sim = 0\n                    for child_name in current.children:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(level, child_name)\n                        if sim > best_sim:\n                            best_sim = sim\n                            best_child = child_name\n                    if best_child and best_sim > 0.3:\n                        path.append(best_child)\n                        current = current.children[best_child]\n                    else:\n                        break\n\n                return path\n\n        return [original_levels[0]] if original_levels[0] else None\n\n    def ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure siblings have less than 30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Checking sibling distinctiveness...\")\n        self._check_sibling_overlap(self.root)\n\n    def _check_sibling_overlap(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Check and fix sibling overlap at a node.\"\"\"\n        siblings = list(node.children.keys())\n\n        for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n            for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                overlap = calculate_word_overlap(sib1, sib2)\n                if overlap > MAX_SIBLING_OVERLAP:\n                    # Try to differentiate\n                    self._differentiate_siblings(node, sib1, sib2)\n\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._check_sibling_overlap(child)\n\n    def _differentiate_siblings(self, parent, name1, name2):\n        \"\"\"Make two sibling names more distinct.\"\"\"\n        # Get unique words from each\n        words1 = get_word_set(name1)\n        words2 = get_word_set(name2)\n\n        unique1 = words1 - words2\n        unique2 = words2 - words1\n\n        # Rename the one with more unique words\n        if unique2 and len(unique2) > len(unique1):\n            new_name = WORD_SEPARATOR.join([w.title() for w in list(unique2)[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME]])\n            if new_name and new_name != name2:\n                self._rename_node(parent, name2, new_name)\n\n    def _rename_node(self, parent, old_name, UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder:\n    \"\"\"Builds and manages the unified taxonomy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = TaxonomyNode('ROOT', 0)\n        self.all_records = []\n\n    def        if not filtered:\n            return child_name\n        return ' '.join(filtered)\n\n    def balance_taxonomy(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance the taxonomy to meet hierarchy constraints.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Balancing taxonomy...\")\n\n        # First, consolidate top-level categories to meet 10-20 constraint\n        self._consolidate_top_level()\n\n        # Then balance each subtree\n        self._balance_subtree(self.root)\n\n        # Update records with new unified levels\n        self._update_records()\n\n    def _consolidate_top_level(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure top level has MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n            # Merge small categories into larger ones\n            sizes = {k: len(v.get_all_descendants()) + 1\n                    for k, v in self.root.children.items()}\n            sorted_cats = sorted(sizes.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n            while len(self.root.children) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n                # Find smallest category and merge with most similar\n                smallest = sorted_cats[0][0]\n                if smallest not in self.root.children:\n                    sorted_cats.pop(0)\n                    continue\n\n                # Find best merge target\n                best_target = None\n                best_similarity = -1\n                for other in self.root.children:\n                    if other != smallest:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(smallest, other)\n                        if sim > best_similarity:\n                            best_similarity = sim\n                            best_target = other\n\n                if best_target:\n                    # Merge smallest into best_target\n                    self._merge_nodes(self.root, smallest, best_target)\n                    sorted_cats.pop(0)\n\n        print(f\"Final top-level categories: {len(self.root.children)}\")\n\n    def _merge_nodes(self, parent, source_name, target_name):\n        \"\"\"Merge source node into target node.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in parent.children or target_name not in parent.children:\n            return\n\n        source = parent.children[source_name]\n        target = parent.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge children\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                # Recursively merge\n                self._merge_child_trees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                child_node.level = del parent.children[source_name]\n\n    def _merge_child_trees(self, target, source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source tree into target tree.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        target.original_paths.extend(source.original_paths)\n\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_child_trees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _balance_subtree(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Balance a subtree to meet constraints.\"\"\"\n        if not node.children:\n            return\n\n        # If too many children, cluster them\n        if len(node.children) > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and node.level < MAX_LEVELS - 1:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # If too few children (and not at max level), may need to promote grandchildren\n        # But we keep it simple for now\n\n        # Recurse\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_subtree(child)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children to reduce count.\"\"\"\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n\n        # Group by first word similarity\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            first_word = name.split()[0] if name else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping helps, restructure\n        if len(groups) < len(children) and len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            new_children = {}\n            for group_name, members in groups.items():\n                if len(members) == 1:\n                    # Single member, keep as is\n                    new_children[members[0][0]] = members[0][1]\n                else:\n                    # Create intermediate node\n                    intermediate_name = self._create_representative_name(\n                        [m[0] for m in members])\n                    intermediate = TaxonomyNode(intermediate_name, parent.level + 1)\n                    for name, node in members:\n                        node.level = intermediate.level + 1\n                        intermediate.children[name] = node\n                        intermediate.sources.update(node.sources)\n                    new_children[intermediate_name] = intermediate\n\n            parent.children = new_children\n\n    def _create_representative_name(self, names, max_words=MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME):\n        \"\"\"Create a representative name for a group of categories.\"\"\"\n        word_counter = Counter()\n        total = len(names)\n\n        for name in names:\n            words = get_word_set(name)\n            word_counter.update(words)\n\n        # Get words appearing in 70%+ of names\n        representative = []\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n                if len(representative) >= max_words:\n                    break\n\n        # Fallback\n        if not representative:\n            representative = [w.title() for w, _ in word_counter.most_common(min(3, max_words))]\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:max_words]) if representative else names[0]\n\n    def _update_records(self):\n        \"\"\"Update all records with current taxonomy structure.\"\"\"\n        # Build path lookup\n        path_map = {}\n        self._build_path_map(self.root, [], path_map)\n\n        # Update each record\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            # Find the path in current taxonomy\n            current = self.root\n            new_levels = []\n\n            for level in record['unified_levels']:\n                if not level or level not in current.children:\n                    break\n                current = current.children[level]\n                new_levels.append(level)\n\n            # If path was modified, try to find closest match\n            if not new_levels:\n                # Find by source path similarity\n                best_match = self._find_best_match(record['unified_levels'])\n                if best_match:\n                    new_levels = best_match\n\n            # Pad to 5 levels\n            while len(new_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n                new_levels.append('')\n\n            record['unified_levels'] = new_levels\n\n    def _build_path_map(self, node, path, result):\n        \"\"\"Build a map of all paths in taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if node.level > 0:\n            result[tuple(path)] = node\n        for name, child in node.children.items():\n            self._build_path_map(child, path + [name], result)\n\n    def _find_best_match(self, original_levels):\n        \"\"\"Find best matching path in current taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if not original_levels or not original_levels[0]:\n            return None\n\n        # Start from top level\n        for top_name in self.root.children:\n            if calculate_word_overlap(original_levels[0], top_name) > 0.5:\n                path = [top_name]\n                current = self.root.children[top_name]\n\n                for i, level in enumerate(original_levels[1:], 1):\n                    if not level:\n                        break\n                    best_child = None\n                    best_sim = 0\n                    for child_name in current.children:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(level, child_name)\n                        if sim > best_sim:\n                            best_sim = sim\n                            best_child = child_name\n                    if best_child and best_sim > 0.3:\n                        path.append(best_child)\n                        current = current.children[best_child]\n                    else:\n                        break\n\n                return path\n\n        return [original_levels[0]] if original_levels[0] else None\n\n    def ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure siblings have less than 30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Checking sibling distinctiveness...\")\n        self._check_sibling_overlap(self.root)\n\n    def _check_sibling_overlap(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Check and fix sibling overlap at a node.\"\"\"\n        siblings = list(node.children.keys())\n\n        for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n            for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                overlap = calculate_word_overlap(sib1, sib2)\n                if overlap > MAX_SIBLING_OVERLAP:\n                    # Try to differentiate\n                    self._differentiate_siblings(node, sib1, sib2)\n\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._check_sibling_overlap(child)\n\n    def _differentiate_siblings(self, parent, name1, name2):\n        \"\"\"Make two sibling names more distinct.\"\"\"\n        # Get unique words from each\n        words1 = get_word_set(name1)\n        words2 = get_word_set(name2)\n\n        unique1 = words1 - words2\n        unique2 = words2 - words1\n\n        # Rename the one with more unique words\n        if unique2 and len(unique2) > len(unique1):\n            new_name = WORD_SEPARATOR.join([w.title() for w in list(unique2)[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME]])\n            if new_name and new_name != name2:\n                self._rename_node(parent, name2, new_name)\n\n    def _rename_node(self, parent, old_name, new_name):\n        \"\"\"Rename a child node.\"\"\"\n        if old_name in parent.children and new_name not in parent.children:\n            node = parent.children[old_name]\n            node.name = new_name\n            parent.children[new_name] = node\n            del parent.children[old_name]\n\n    def check_source_distribution(self):\n        \"\"\"Check and report source distribution across taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nSource distribution in top-level categories:\")\n        for name, node in sorted(self.root.children.items()):\n            source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n            for child in [node] + node.get_all_descendants():\n                for source in child.sources:\n                    source_counts[source] += 1\n\n            total = sum(source_counts.values())\n            dist = {s: f\"{c/total:.1%}\" for s, c in source_counts.items()}\n            print(f\"  {name}: {dist}\")\n\n    def generate_full_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the full taxonomy DataFrame.\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            row = {\n                'source': record['source'],\n                'category_path': record['category_path'],\n                'depth': record['depth'],\n            }\n            for i, level in enumerate(record['unified_levels'], 1):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i}'] = level if level else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n        return df\n\n    def generate_hierarchy_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the hierarchy DataFrame.\"\"\"\n        paths = set()\n\n        def collect_paths(node, current_path):\n            if node.level > 0:\n                # Add all partial paths\n                for i in range(len(current_path)):\n                    paths.add(tuple(current_path[:i+1]))\n\n            for child_name, child in node.children.items():\n                collect_paths(child, current_path + [child_name])\n\n        collect_paths(self.root, [])\n\n        # Convert to rows\n        rows = []\n        for path in sorted(paths):\n            row = {}\n            for i in range(MAX_LEVELS):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i+1}'] = path[i] if i < len(path) else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n        df = df.sort_values(\n            by=[f'unified_level_{i}' for i in range(1, MAX_LEVELS + 1)]\n        ).reset_index(drop=True)\n\n        return df\n\n    def print_statistics(self):\n        \"\"\"Print taxonomy statistics.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(\"UNIFIED TAXONOMY STATISTICS\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Top level count\n        print(f\"\\nTop-level categories: {len(self.root.children)}\")\n\n        # Source distribution\n        source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            source_counts[record['source']] += 1\n        print(f\"\\nRecords by source:\")\n        for source, count in sorted(source_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  {source}: {count}\")\n\n        # Depth distribution\n        depth_counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            depth_counts[record['depth']] += 1\n        print(f\"\\nRecords by depth:\")\n        for depth, count in sorted(depth_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  Level {depth}: {count}\")\n\n        # Children per level\n        def count_at_level(node, target_level, current_level=0):\n            if current_level == target_level:\n                return [len(node.children)] if node.children else []\n            counts = []\n            for child in node.children.values():\n                counts.extend(count_at_level(child, target_level, current_level + 1))\n            return counts\n\n        print(f\"\\nChildren distribution:\")\n        for level in range(1, MAX_LEVELS):\n            counts = count_at_level(self.root, level)\n            if counts:\n                avg = sum(counts) / len(counts)\n                print(f\"  Level {level}: avg {avg:.1f} children (range: {min(counts)}-{max(counts)})\")\n\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading category data...\")\n\n    # Load all data\n    amazon_df = load_amazon_data()\n parse_category_path(row['category_path'])\n        if levels:\n            builder.add_category('google', row['category_path'], levels)\n\n    # Balance taxonomy\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 40)\n    builder.balance_taxonomy()\n\n    # Ensure sibling distinctiveness\n    builder.ensure_sibling_distinctiveness()\n\n    # Check source distribution\n    builder.check_source_distribution()\n\n    # Print statistics\n    builder.print_statistics()\n\n    # Generate outputs\n    print(\"\\nGenerating output files...\")\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Full taxonomy\n \"\"\"Parse a category path string into a list of levels.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(path):\n        return []\n    path = str(path).strip()\n    # Handle both \" > \" and \">\" separators\n    if ' > ' in path:\n        levels = [level.strip() for level in path.split(' > ')]\n    else:\n        levels = [level.strip() for level in path.split('>')]\n    return [l for l in levels if l]\n\n# normalize_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize category text for consistency.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return ''\n    text = str(text).strip()\n    # Title case\n    text = text.title()\n    # Clean extra whitespace\n    text = ' '.join(text.split())\n    return text\n\ndef get_word_set(text):\n    \"\"\"Get set of meaningful words from text.\"\"\"\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', '&', 'for', 'of', 'in', 'with', 'to', 'a', 'an'}\n    words = re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b', text.lower())\n    return set(w for w in words if w not in stop_words and len(w) > 1)\n\ndef calculate_word_overlap(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage between two texts.\"\"\"\n    words1 = get_word_set(text1)\n    words2 = get_word_set(text2)\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    min_size = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n    return len(intersection) / min_size if min_size > 0 else 0.0\n\n# 15 standard top-level categories (target within 10-20 range)\nSTANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES = [\n    'Electronics | Technology',\n    'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'Home | Garden',\n    'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'Toys | Games',\n    'Food | Beverages',\n    'Health | Household',\n    'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'Books | Media',\n    'Office | Supplies',\n    'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'Pet Supplies',\n    'Baby | Kids',\n    'Arts | Crafts',\n]\n\n# Map source category names to standard top-level names\nTOP_LEVEL_MAPPING = {\n    # Electronics\n    'electronics': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'consumer electronics': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'video games': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'computers': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'camera & photo': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'cell phones & accessories': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'cameras & optics': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'software': 'Electronics | Technology',\n\n    # Clothing\n    'clothing': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'clothing, shoes & jewelry': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'fashion': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'apparel & accessories': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"women's fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"men's fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"girls' fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"boys' fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'clothing & accessories': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'jewelry & watches': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n\n    # Home & Garden\n    'home & kitchen': 'Home | Garden',\n    'home & garden': 'Home | Garden',\n    'furniture': 'Home | Garden',\n    'garden & outdoor': 'Home | Garden',\n    'patio, lawn & garden': 'Home | Garden',\n    'tools & home improvement': 'Home | Garden',\n    'kitchen & dining': 'Home | Garden',\n    'home': 'Home | Garden',\n    'home improvement': 'Home | Garden',\n    'patio & garden': 'Home | Garden',\n    'appliances': 'Home | Garden',\n\n    # Health & Beauty\n    'health & household': 'Health | Household',\n    'health & beauty': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'beauty & personal care': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'beauty': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'personal care': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n\n    # Food & Beverages\n    'grocery & gourmet food': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'food & beverages': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'food': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'beverages': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'tobacco': 'Food | Beverages',\n\n    # Sports & Outdoors\n    'sports & outdoors': 'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'sporting goods': 'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'outdoor recreation': 'Sports | Outdoors',\n\n    # Books & Media\n    'books': 'Books | Media',\n    'movies & tv': 'Books | Media',\n    'cds & vinyl': 'Books | Media',\n    'digital music': 'Books | Media',\n    'media': 'Books | Media',\n    'arts & entertainment': 'Books | Media',\n    'antiques & collectibles': 'Books | Media',\n\n    # Pet\n    'pet supplies': 'Pet Supplies',\n    'animals & pet supplies': 'Pet Supplies',\n\n    # Baby\n    'baby': 'Baby | Kids',\n    'baby products': 'Baby | Kids',\n    'baby & toddler': 'Baby | Kids',\n\n    # Office\n    'office products': 'Office | Supplies',\n    'office supplies': 'Office | Supplies',\n    'office': 'Office | Supplies',\n\n    # Industrial\n    'industrial & scientific': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'business & industrial': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'hardware': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n\n    # Arts\n    'arts, crafts & sewing': 'Arts | Crafts',\n    'arts & crafts': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Automotive\n    'automotive': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'vehicles & parts': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'auto parts & accessories': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n\n    # Toys\n    'toys & games': 'Toys | Games',\n    'toys': 'Toys | Games',\n    'games': 'Toys | Games',\n\n    # Travel/Luggage -> map to Clothing\n    'luggage & travel gear': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'luggage & bags': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'travel & luggage': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n\n    # Musical Instruments -> Arts\n    'musical instruments': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Mature\n    'mature': 'Health | Household',\n\n    # Religious -> Arts\n    'religious items & ceremonial': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Other\n    'other': 'Other',\n}\n\ndef map_to_top_level(category_name):\n    \"\"\"Map a category name to a standardized top-level category.\"\"\"\n    if not category_name:\n        return 'Other'\n    key = category_name.lower().strip()\n    return TOP_LEVEL_MAPPING.get(key, None)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TAXONOMY NODE\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass TaxonomyNode:\n    \"\"\"Represents a node in the taxonomy hierarchy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, name, level=0):\n        self.name = name\n        self.level = level\n        self.children = {}\n        self.sources = set()\n        self.original_paths = []\n        self.record_refs = []  # References to records for updating\n\n    def add_child(self, name):\n        \"\"\"Add or get a child node.\"\"\"\n        if name not in self.children:\n            self.children[name] = TaxonomyNode(name, self.level + 1)\n        return self.children[name]\n\n    def get_child_count(self):\n        \"\"\"Get number of children.\"\"\"\n        return len(self.children)\n\n    def get_all_descendants(self):\n        \"\"\"Get all descendant nodes.\"\"\"\n        descendants = list(self.children.values())\n        for child in list(self.children.values()):\n            descendants.extend(child.get_all_descendants())\n        return descendants\n\n# ============================================================================\n# UNIFIED TAXONOMY BUILDER\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder:\n    \"\"\"Builds and manages the unified taxonomy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = TaxonomyNode('ROOT', 0)\n        self.all_records = []\n\n    def add_category(self, source, original_path, levels):\n        \"\"\"Add a category path to the taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if not levels:\n            return\n\n        # Map first level to standardized top-level category\n        top_level = map_to_top_level(levels[0])\n\n        if top_level is None:\n            # Try partial match\n            key = levels[0].lower().strip()\n            for pattern, mapped in TOP_LEVEL_MAPPING.items():\n                if pattern in key or key in pattern:\n                    top_level = mapped\n                    break\n\n            if top_level is None:\n                # Use normalized original as fallback\n                top_level = normalize_text(levels[0])\n\n        unified_levels = [top_level]\n\n        # Process remaining levels (levels 2-5)\n        for i, level in enumerate(levels[1:MAX_LEVELS], 1):\n            normalized = normalize_text(level)\n            # Remove parent word overlap to avoid redundancy\n            normalized = self._remove_parent_overlap(normalized, unified_levels[-1])\n            if normalized:\n                unified_levels.append(normalized)\n            else:\n                unified_levels.append(normalize_text(level))\n\n        # Pad to 5 levels\n        while len(unified_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n            unified_levels.append('')\n\n        # Only keep first 5 levels\n        unified_levels = unified_levels[:MAX_LEVELS]\n\n        # Add to tree\n        current = self.root\n        for i, level_name in enumerate(unified_levels):\n            if not level_name:\n                break\n            current = current.add_child(level_name)\n            current.sources.add(source)\n\n        # Store record\n        record = {\n            'source': source,\n            'category_path': original_path,\n            'depth': min(len([l for l in levels if l]), MAX_LEVELS),\n            'unified_levels': unified_levels.copy()\n        }\n        self.all_records.append(record)\n\n    def _remove_parent_overlap(self, child_name, parent_name):\n        \"\"\"Remove words from child that appear in parent.\"\"\"\n        parent_words = get_word_set(parent_name)\n        child_words = child_name.split()\n        filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n        if not filtered:\n            return child_name\n        return ' '.join(filtered)\n\n    def consolidate_top_level(self):\n        \"\"\"Consolidate top-level categories.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nConsolidating top-level categories...\")\n        print(f\"Before: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n        # Find categories that should be merged\n        current_cats = list(self.root.children.keys())\n\n        # Merge any unmapped categories into closest standard category\n        for cat in current_cats:\n            if cat not in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES and cat != 'Other':\n                # Find best match from standard categories\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for std_cat in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES:\n                    if std_cat in self.root.children:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, std_cat)\n                        if sim > best_sim:\n                            best_sim = sim\n                            best_match = std_cat\n\n                # If good match found, merge\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    self._merge_top_level(cat, best_match)\n                elif 'Other' in current_cats or cat == 'Other':\n                    pass  # Keep as is\n                else:\n                    # Find any similar category\n                    for other_cat in list(self.root.children.keys()):\n                        if other_cat != cat:\n                            sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, other_cat)\n                            if sim > 0.4:\n                                # Merge into the larger one\n                                size_cat = len(self.root.children[cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                size_other = len(self.root.children[other_cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                if size_cat > size_other:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(other_cat, cat)\n                                else:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(cat, other_cat)\n                                break\n\n        # If still too many, merge smallest\n        while len(self.root.children) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n            sizes = {k: len(v.get_all_descendants()) + 1\n                    for k, v in self.root.children.items()}\n            smallest = min(sizes, key=sizes.get)\n\n            # Find most similar category to merge with\n            best_match = None\n            best_sim = 0\n            for other in self.root.children:\n                if other != smallest:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(smallest, other)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = other\n\n            if best_match:\n                self._merge_top_level(smallest, best_match)\n            else:\n                # Merge with largest for balance\n                del self.root.children[smallest]\n\n        print(f\"After: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n    def _merge_top_level(self, source_name, target_name):\n        \"\"\"Merge source top-level category into target.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in self.root.children or target_name not in self.root.children:\n            return\n        if source_name == target_name:\n            return\n\n        source = self.root.children[source_name]\n        target = self.root.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge all children\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                self._update_levels(child_node, target.level + 1)\n\n        # Merge metadata\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n\n        # Update all records that used source_name\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            if record['unified_levels'][0] == source_name:\n                record['unified_levels'][0] = target_name\n\n        # Remove source\n        del self.root.children[source_name]\n        print(f\"  Merged '{source_name}' into '{target_name}'\")\n\n    def _merge_subtrees(self, target, source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _update_levels(self, node, new_level):\n        \"\"\"Update level of node and all descendants.\"\"\"\n        node.level = new_level\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._update_levels(child, new_level + 1)\n\n    def balance_subtrees(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance all subtrees to meet 3-20 children constraint.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBalancing subtrees...\")\n        self._balance_node(self.root, max_depth=MAX_LEVELS)\n\n    def _balance_node(self, node, max_depth):\n        \"\"\"Recursively balance a node's children.\"\"\"\n        if node.level >= max_depth:\n            return\n\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if num_children > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # Recurse to children\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_node(child, max_depth)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children when there are too many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n\n        # Group by first significant word\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            first_word = words[0] if words else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping helps enough\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < len(children):\n            new_children = {}\n            for group_name, members in groups.items():\n                if len(members) == 1:\n                    new_children[members[0][0]] = members[0][1]\n                else:\n                    # Create grouped category name\n                    member_names = [m[0] for m in members]\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, group_name)\n\n                    # Create intermediate node\n                    intermediate = TaxonomyNode(group_cat_name, parent.level + 1)\n                    for name, node in members:\n                        # Shorten child name by removing group words\n                        shortened = self._remove_parent_overlap(name, group_cat_name)\n                        if shortened and shortened != name:\n                            intermediate.children[shortened] = node\n                        else:\n                            intermediate.children[name] = node\n                        self._update_levels(node, intermediate.level + 1)\n                        intermediate.sources.update(node.sources)\n\n                    new_children[group_cat_name] = intermediate\n\n            parent.children = new_children\n\n            # Update records\n            self._update_records_for_grouping(parent)\n\n    def _create_group_name(self, member_names, hint):\n        \"\"\"Create a representative name for a group.\"\"\"\n        word_counter = Counter()\n        for name in member_names:\n            words = get_word_set(name)\n            word_counter.update(words)\n\n        total = len(member_names)\n        representative = []\n\n        # First add the hint word\n        representative.append(hint.title())\n\n        # Add words appearing in 70%+ of members\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if word.lower() != hint.lower() and count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n            if len(representative) >= MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME:\n                break\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME])\n\n    def _update_records_for_grouping(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Update records after grouping (no-op for now, handled at output).\"\"\"\n        pass\n\n    def ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure siblings have less than 30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nChecking sibling distinctiveness...\")\n        self._check_sibling_overlap(self.root)\n\n    def _check_sibling_overlap(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Check and fix sibling overlap at a node.\"\"\"\n        siblings = list(node.children.keys())\n        renamed = {}\n\n        for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n            for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                if sib1 in renamed:\n                    sib1 = renamed[sib1]\n                if sib2 in renamed:\n                    sib2 = renamed[sib2]\n\n                overlap = calculate_word_overlap(sib1, sib2)\n                if overlap > MAX_SIBLING_OVERLAP:\n                    # Differentiate by adding unique words\n                    words1 = get_word_set(sib1)\n                    words2 = get_word_set(sib2)\n                    unique2 = words2 - words1\n\n                    if unique2:\n                        new_name = WORD_SEPARATOR.join([w.title() for w in list(unique2)[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME]])\n                        if new_name and new_name != sib2:\n                            self._rename_child(node, sib2, new_name)\n                            renamed[sib2] = new_name\n\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._check_sibling_overlap(child)\n\n    def _rename_child(self, parent, old_name, new_name):\n        \"\"\"Rename a child node.\"\"\"\n        if old_name in parent.children and new_name not in parent.children:\n            node = parent.children[old_name]\n            node.name = new_name\n            parent.children[new_name] = node\n            del parent.children[old_name]\n\n    def rebuild_records(self):\n        \"\"\"Rebuild all records to match current tree structure.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nRebuilding records to match tree...\")\n\n        # Build lookup from original path -> current tree path\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            current_path = self._find_path_in_tree(record['unified_levels'])\n            if current_path:\n                # Pad to 5 levels\n                while len(current_path) < MAX_LEVELS:\n                    current_path.append('')\n                record['unified_levels'] = current_path[:MAX_LEVELS]\n\n    def _find_path_in_tree(self, original_levels):\n        \"\"\"Find the current path in tree for original unified levels.\"\"\"\n        path = []\n        current = self.root\n\n        for level in original_levels:\n            if not level:\n                break\n\n            if level in current.children:\n                path.append(level)\n                current = current.children[level]\n            else:\n                # Try to find best match\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for child_name in current.children:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(level, child_name)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = child_name\n\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    path.append(best_match)\n                    current = current.children[best_match]\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        return path if path else [original_levels[0]] if original_levels and original_levels[0] else []\n\n    def check_source_distribution(self):\n        \"\"\"Check and report source distribution across taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nSource distribution in top-level categories:\")\n        for name, node in sorted(self.root.children.items()):\n            source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n            all_nodes = [node] + node.get_all_descendants()\n            for n in all_nodes:\n                for source in n.sources:\n                    source_counts[source] += 1\n\n            total = sum(source_counts.values())\n            if total > 0:\n                dist = {s: f\"{c/total:.1%}\" for s, c in sorted(source_counts.items())}\n                print(f\"  {name}: {dist}\")\n\n    def generate_full_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the full taxonomy DataFrame.\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            row = {\n                'source': record['source'],\n                'category_path': record['category_path'],\n                'depth': record['depth'],\n            }\n            for i, level in enumerate(record['unified_levels'], 1):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i}'] = level if level else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\n        # Ensure column order\n        cols = ['source', 'category_path', 'depth',\n                'unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2', 'unified_level_3',\n                'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5']\n        return df[cols]\n\n    def generate_hierarchy_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the hierarchy DataFrame with all unique paths.\"\"\"\n        paths = set()\n\n        def collect_paths(node, current_path):\n            if node.level > 0:\n                # Add all partial paths (from level 1 to current)\n                for i in range(len(current_path)):\n                    paths.add(tuple(current_path[:i+1]))\n\n            for child_name, child in node.children.items():\n                collect_paths(child, current_path + [child_name])\n\n        collect_paths(self.root, [])\n\n        # Convert to rows\n        rows = []\n        for path in sorted(paths):\n            row = {}\n            for i in range(MAX_LEVELS):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i+1}'] = path[i] if i < len(path) else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n        df = df.sort_values(\n            by=[f'unified_level_{i}' for i in range(1, MAX_LEVELS + 1)]\n        ).reset_index(drop=True)\n\n        return df\n\n    def print_statistics(self):\n        \"\"\"Print taxonomy statistics.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(\"UNIFIED TAXONOMY STATISTICS\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Top level count\n        print(f\"\\nTop-level categories: {len(self.root.children)}\")\n        for cat in sorted(self.root.children.keys()):\n            desc_count = len(self.root.children[cat].get_all_descendants())\n            print(f\"  - {cat}: {desc_count} descendants\")\n\n        # Source distribution\n        source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            source_counts[record['source']] += 1\n        print(f\"\\nRecords by source:\")\n        for source, count in sorted(source_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  {source}: {count}\")\n\n        # Depth distribution\n        depth_counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            depth_counts[record['depth']] += 1\n        print(f\"\\nRecords by depth:\")\n        for depth, count in sorted(depth_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  Level {depth}: {count}\")\n\n        # Children per level\n        def count_at_level(node, target_level, current_level=0):\n            if current_level == target_level:\n                return [len(node.children)] if node.children else []\n            counts = []\n            for child in node.children.values():\n                counts.extend(count_at_level(child, target_level, current_level + 1))\n            return counts\n\n        print(f\"\\nChildren distribution:\")\n        for level in range(1, MAX_LEVELS):\n            counts = count_at_level(self.root, level)\n            counts = [c for c in counts if c > 0]\n            if counts:\n                avg = sum(counts) / len(counts)\n                min_c, max_c = min(counts), max(counts)\n                print(f\"  Level {level}: avg {avg:.1f} children (range: {min_c}-{max_c})\")\n\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading category data...\")\n\n    # Load all data\n    amazon_df = load_amazon_data()\n    facebook_df = load_facebook_data()\n    google_df = load_google_data()\n\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_df)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(facebook_df)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_df)} categories\")\n\n    # Initialize builder\n    builder = UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder()\n\n    # Process Amazon\n    print(\"\\nProcessing Amazon categories...\")\n    for _, row in amazon_df.iterrows():\n        levels = parse_category_path(row['category_path'])\n        if levels:\n            builder.add_category('amazon', row['category_path'], levels)\n\n    # Process Facebook\n    print(\"Processing Facebook categories...\")\n    for _, row in facebook_df.iterrows():\n        levels = parse_category_path(row['category_path'])\n        if levels:\n            builder.add_category('facebook', row['category_path'], levels)\n\n    # Process Google\n    print(\"Processing Google categories...\")\n    for _, row in google_df.iterrows():\n        levels = parse_category_path(row['category_path'])\n        if levels:\n            builder.add_category('google', row['category_path'], levels)\n\n    # Consolidate top-level categories\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 40)\n    builder.consolidate_top_level()\n\n    # Balance subtrees\n    builder.balance_subtrees()\n\n    # Ensure sibling distinctiveness\n    builder.ensure_sibling_distinctiveness()\n\n    # Rebuild records to match current tree\n    builder.rebuild_records()\n\n    # Check source distribution\n    builder.check_source_distribution()\n\n    # Print statistics\n    builder.print_statistics()\n\n    # Generate outputs\n    print(\"\\nGenerating output files...\")\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Full taxonomy\n    full_df = builder.generate_full_output()\n    full_path = f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/unified_taxonomy_full.csv'\n    full_df.to_csv(full_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"  {full_path}: {len(full_df)} rows\")\n\n    # Hierarchy\n    hierarchy_df = builder.generate_hierarchy_output()\n    hierarchy_path = f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/unified_taxonomy_hierarchy.csv'\n    hierarchy_df.to_csv(hierarchy_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"  {hierarchy_path}: {len(hierarchy_df)} rows\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(\"\\nValidation:\")\n    top_level_count = len(builder.root.children)\n    print(f\"  Top-level categories: None:\n                # Use normalized original as fallback\n                top_level = normalize_text(levels[0])\n\n        unified_levels = [top_level]\n\n        # Process remaining levels (levels 2-5)\n        for i, level in enumerate(levels[1:MAX_LEVELS], 1):\n            normalized = normalize_text(level)\n            # Remove parent word overlap to avoid redundancy\n            normalized = self._remove_parent_overlap(normalized, unified_levels[-1])\n            if normalized:\n                unified_levels.append(normalized)\n            else:\n                unified_levels.append(normalize_text(level))\n\n        # Pad to 5 levels\n        while len(unified_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n            unified_levels.append('')\n\n        # Only keep first 5 levels\n        unified_levels = unified_levels[:MAX_LEVELS]\n\n        # Add to tree\n        current = self.root\n        for i, level_name in enumerate(unified_levels):\n            if not level_name:\n                break\n            current = current.add_child(level_name)\n            current.sources.add(source)\n\n        # Store record\n        record = {\n            'source': source,\n            'category_path': original_path,\n            'depth': min(len([l for l in levels if l]), MAX_LEVELS),\n            'unified_levels': unified_levels.copy()\n        }\n        self.all_records.append(record)\n\n    def _remove_parent_overlap(self, child_name, parent_name):\n        \"\"\"Remove words from child that appear in parent.\"\"\"\n        parent_words = get_word_set(parent_name)\n        child_words = child_name.split()\n        filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n        if not filtered:\n            return child_name\n        return ' '.join(filtered)\n\n    def consolidate_top_level(self):\n        \"\"\"Consolidate top-level categories.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nConsolidating top-level categories...\")\n        print(f\"Before: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n        # Find categories that should be merged\n        current_cats = list(self.root.children.keys())\n\n        # Merge any unmapped categories into closest standard category\n        for cat in current_cats:\n            if cat not in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES and cat != 'Other':\n                # Find best match from standard categories\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for std_cat in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES:\n                    if std_cat in self.root.children:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, std_cat)\n                        if sim > best_sim:\n                            best_sim = sim\n                            best_match = std_cat\n\n                # If good match found, merge\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    self._merge_top_level(cat, best_match)\n                elif 'Other' in current_cats or cat == 'Other':\n                    pass  # Keep as is\n                else:\n                    # Find any similar category\n                    for other_cat in                        else:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(cat, other_cat)\n                                break\n\n        # If still too many, merge smallest\n        while len(self.root.children) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n            sizes = {k: len(v.get_all_descendants()) + 1\n                    for k, v in self.root.children.items()}\n            smallest = min(sizes, key=sizes.get)\n\n            # Find most similar category to merge with\n            best_match = None\n            best_sim = 0\n            for other in self.root.children:\n                if other != smallest:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(smallest, other)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = other\n\n            if best_match:\n                self._merge_top_level(smallest, best_match)\n            else:\n                # Merge with largest for balance\n                del category into target.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in self.root.children or target_name not in self.root.children:\n            return\n        if source_name == target_name:\n            return\n\n        source = self.root.children[source_name]\n        target = self.root.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge all children\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                self._update_levels(child_node, target.level + 1)\n\n        # Merge metadata\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n\n        # Update all records that used source_name\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            if record['unified_levels'][0] == source_name:\n                record['unified_levels'][0] = source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _update_levels(self, node, new_level):\n        \"\"\"Update level of node and all descendants.\"\"\"\n        node.level = new_level\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._update_levels(child, new_level + 1)\n\n    def balance_subtrees(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance all subtrees to meet 3-20 children constraint.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBalancing subtrees...\")\n        self._balance_node(self.root, max_depth=MAX_LEVELS)\n\n    def _balance_node(self, node, max_depth):\n        \"\"\"Recursively balance a node's children.\"\"\"\n        if node.level >= max_depth:\n            return\n\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if num_children > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # Recurse to children\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_node(child, max_depth)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children when there are too many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n\n        # Group by first significant word\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            first_word = words[0] if words else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping helps enough\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < len(children):\n            new_children = {}\n            for group_name, members in groups.items():\n                if len(members) == 1:\n                    new_children[members[0][0]] = members[0][1]\n                else:\n                    # Create grouped category name\n                    member_names = [m[0] for m in members]\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, group_name)\n\n                    # Create intermediate node\n                    intermediate = TaxonomyNode(group_cat_name, parent.level + 1)\n                    for name, node in members:\n                        # Shorten child name by removing group words\n                        shortened = self._remove_parent_overlap(name, group_cat_name)\n                        if shortened and shortened != name:\n                            intermediate.children[shortened] = node\n                        else:\n                            intermediate.children[name] = node\n parent.children = new_children\n\n            # Update records\n            self._update_records_for_grouping(parent)\n\n    def _create_group_name(self, member_names, hint):\n        \"\"\"Create a representative name for a group.\"\"\"\n        word_counter = Counter()\n        for name in member_names:\n            words = get_word_set(name)\n            word_counter.update(words)\n\n        total = len(member_names)\n        representative = []\n\n        # First add the hint word\n        representative.append(hint.title())\n\n        # Add words appearing in 70%+ of members\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if word.lower() != hint.lower() and count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n            if len(representative) >= MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME:\n                break\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME])\n\n    def _update_records_for_grouping(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Update records after grouping (no-op for now, handled at output).\"\"\"\n        pass\n\n    def ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure siblings have less than 30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nChecking sibling distinctiveness...\")\n        self._check_sibling_overlap(self.root)\n\n    def _check_sibling_overlap(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Check and fix sibling overlap at a node.\"\"\"\n        siblings = list(node.children.keys())\n        renamed = {}\n\n        for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n            for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                if sib1 in renamed:\n                    sib1 = renamed[sib1]\n                if sib2 in renamed:\n                    sib2 = renamed[sib2]\n\n                overlap \"\"\"Parse a category path string into a list of levels.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(path):\n        return []\n    path = str(path).strip()\n    # Handle both \" > \" and \">\" separators\n    if ' > ' in normalize_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize category text for consistency.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return ''\n    text = str(text).strip()\n    # Title case\n    text = UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder:\n    \"\"\"Builds and manages the unified taxonomy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = TaxonomyNode('ROOT', 0)\n        self.all_records = []\n\n    def add_category(self, source, original_path, levels):\n        \"\"\"Add a category path to the taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if not levels:\n            return\n\n        # Map first level to standardized    unified_levels.append(normalized)\n\n        # Pad to 5 levels\n        while len(unified_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n            unified_levels.append('')\n\n        # Add to tree\n        current = target node.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in parent.children or target_name not in parent.children:\n            return\n\n        source = parent.children[source_name]\n        target = _balance_subtree(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Balance a subtree to meet constraints.\"\"\"\n        if not node.children:\n            return\n\n        # If too many children, cluster them\n        if _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children to reduce count.\"\"\"\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n\n        # Group by first word similarity\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            first_word = name.split()[0] if name else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping       representative = []\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n                if len(representative) >= max_words:\n                    break\n\n        # Fallback\n        if not representative:\n            representative = [w.title() for w, _ in word_counter.most_common(min(3, max_words))]\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:max_words]) if representative else names[0]\n\n    def _update_records(self):\n        \"\"\"Update normalized = self._remove_parent_overlap(normalized, unified_levels[-1])\n            if normalized:\n                unified_levels.append(normalized)\n            else:\n source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n\n        # Group by first significant word\n        groups
