Kids',\n    'baby & toddler': 'Baby | Kids',\n\n    # Office\n    'office products': 'Office | Supplies',\n    'office supplies': 'Office | Supplies',\n    'office': 'Office | Supplies',\n\n    # Industrial\n    'industrial & scientific': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'business & industrial': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'hardware': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n\n    # Arts\n    'arts, crafts & sewing': 'Arts | Crafts',\n    'arts & crafts': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Automotive\n    'automotive': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'vehicles & parts': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'auto parts & accessories': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n\n    # Toys\n    'toys & games': 'Toys | Games',\n    'toys': 'Toys | Games',\n    'games': 'Toys | Games',\n\n    # Travel/Luggage -> map to Clothing\n    'luggage & travel gear': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'luggage & bags': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'travel & luggage': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n\n    # Musical Instruments -> Arts\n    'musical instruments': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Mature\n    'mature': 'Health | Household',\n\n    # Religious -> Arts\n    'religious items & ceremonial': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Other\n    'other': 'Other',\n}\n\ndef map_to_top_level(category_name):\n    \"\"\"Map a category name to a standardized top-level category.\"\"\"\n    if not category_name:\n        return 'Other'\n    key = category_name.lower().strip()\n    return TOP_LEVEL_MAPPING.get(key, None)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TAXONOMY NODE\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass TaxonomyNode:\n    \"\"\"Represents a node in the taxonomy hierarchy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, name, level=0):\n        self.name = name\n        self.level = level\n        self.children = {}\n        self.sources = set()\n        self.original_paths = []\n        self.record_refs = []  # References to records for updating\n\n    def add_child(self, name):\n        \"\"\"Add or get a child node.\"\"\"\n        if name not in self.children:\n            self.children[name] = TaxonomyNode(name, self.level + 1)\n        return self.children[name]\n\n    def get_child_count(self):\n        \"\"\"Get number of children.\"\"\"\n        return len(self.children)\n\n    def get_all_descendants(self):\n        \"\"\"Get all descendant nodes.\"\"\"\n        descendants = list(self.children.values())\n        for child in list(self.children.values()):\n            descendants.extend(child.get_all_descendants())\n        return descendants\n\n# ============================================================================\n# UNIFIED TAXONOMY BUILDER\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder:\n    \"\"\"Builds and manages the unified taxonomy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = TaxonomyNode('ROOT', 0)\n        self.all_records = []\n\n    def add_category(self, source, original_path, levels):\n        \"\"\"Add a category path to the taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if not levels:\n            return\n\n        # Map first level to standardized top-level category\n        top_level = map_to_top_level(levels[0])\n\n        if top_level is None:\n            # Try partial match\n            key = levels[0].lower().strip()\n            for pattern, mapped in TOP_LEVEL_MAPPING.items():\n                if pattern in key or key in pattern:\n                    top_level = mapped\n                    break\n\n            if top_level is None:\n                # Use normalized original as fallback\n                top_level = normalize_text(levels[0])\n\n        unified_levels = [top_level]\n\n        # Process remaining levels (levels 2-5)\n        for i, level in enumerate(levels[1:MAX_LEVELS], 1):\n            normalized = normalize_text(level)\n            # Remove parent word overlap to avoid redundancy\n            normalized = self._remove_parent_overlap(normalized, unified_levels[-1])\n            if normalized:\n                unified_levels.append(normalized)\n            else:\n                unified_levels.append(normalize_text(level))\n\n        # Pad to 5 levels\n        while len(unified_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n            unified_levels.append('')\n\n        # Only keep first 5 levels\n        unified_levels = unified_levels[:MAX_LEVELS]\n\n        # Add to tree\n        current = self.root\n        for i, level_name in enumerate(unified_levels):\n            if not level_name:\n                break\n            current = current.add_child(level_name)\n            current.sources.add(source)\n\n        # Store record\n        record = {\n            'source': source,\n            'category_path': original_path,\n            'depth': min(len([l for l in levels if l]), MAX_LEVELS),\n            'unified_levels': unified_levels.copy()\n        }\n        self.all_records.append(record)\n\n    def _remove_parent_overlap(self, child_name, parent_name):\n        \"\"\"Remove words from child that appear in parent.\"\"\"\n        parent_words = get_word_set(parent_name)\n        child_words = child_name.split()\n        filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n        if not filtered:\n            return child_name\n        return ' '.join(filtered)\n\n    def consolidate_top_level(self):\n        \"\"\"Consolidate top-level categories.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nConsolidating top-level categories...\")\n        print(f\"Before: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n        # First, apply explicit merges from MERGE_CATEGORIES\n        for source_cat, target_cat in MERGE_CATEGORIES.items():\n            if source_cat in self.root.children:\n                if target_cat not in self.root.children:\n                    # Create target if it doesn't exist\n                    self.root.children[target_cat] = TaxonomyNode(target_cat, 1)\n                self._merge_top_level(source_cat, target_cat)\n\n        # Find categories that should be merged\n        current_cats = list(self.root.children.keys())\n\n        # Merge any unmapped categories into closest standard category\n        for cat in current_cats:\n            if cat not in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES and cat != 'Other':\n                # Find best match from standard categories\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for std_cat in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES:\n                    if std_cat in self.root.children:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, std_cat)\n                        if sim > best_sim:\n                            best_sim = sim\n                            best_match = std_cat\n\n                # If good match found, merge\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    self._merge_top_level(cat, best_match)\n                elif 'Other' in current_cats or cat == 'Other':\n                    pass  # Keep as is\n                else:\n                    # Find any similar category\n                    for other_cat in list(self.root.children.keys()):\n                        if other_cat != cat:\n                            sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, other_cat)\n                            if sim > 0.4:\n                                # Merge into the larger one\n                                size_cat = len(self.root.children[cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                size_other = len(self.root.children[other_cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                if size_cat > size_other:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(other_cat, cat)\n                                else:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(cat, other_cat)\n                                break\n\n        # If still too many, merge smallest\n        while len(self.root.children) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n            sizes = {k: len(v.get_all_descendants()) + 1\n                    for k, v in self.root.children.items()}\n            smallest = min(sizes, key=sizes.get)\n\n            # Find most similar category to merge with\n            best_match = None\n            best_sim = 0\n            for other in self.root.children:\n                if other != smallest:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(smallest, other)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = other\n\n            if best_match:\n                self._merge_top_level(smallest, best_match)\n            else:\n                # Merge with largest for balance\n                del self.root.children[smallest]\n\n        print(f\"After: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n    def _merge_top_level(self, source_name, target_name):\n        \"\"\"Merge source top-level category into target.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in self.root.children or target_name not in self.root.children:\n            return\n        if source_name == target_name:\n            return\n\n        source = self.root.children[source_name]\n        target = self.root.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge all children\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                self._update_levels(child_node, target.level + 1)\n\n        # Merge metadata\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n\n        # Update all records that used source_name\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            if record['unified_levels'][0] == source_name:\n                record['unified_levels'][0] = target_name\n\n        # Remove source\n        del self.root.children[source_name]\n        print(f\"  Merged '{source_name}' into '{target_name}'\")\n\n    def _merge_subtrees(self, target, source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _update_levels(self, node, new_level):\n        \"\"\"Update level of node and all descendants.\"\"\"\n        node.level = new_level\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._update_levels(child, new_level + 1)\n\n    def balance_subtrees(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance all subtrees to meet 3-20 children constraint.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBalancing subtrees...\")\n        self._balance_node(self.root, max_depth=MAX_LEVELS)\n\n    def _balance_node(self, node, max_depth):\n        \"\"\"Recursively balance a node's children.\"\"\"\n        if node.level >= max_depth:\n            return\n\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if num_children > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # Recurse to children\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_node(child, max_depth)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children when there are too many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n        num_children = len(children)\n\n        # Strategy 1: Group by first significant word\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            first_word = words[0] if words else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping by first word helps enough\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 2: Group by common word prefix (first 2 words)\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            prefix = ' '.join(words[:2]) if len(words) >= 2 else (words[0] if words else 'Other')\n            groups[prefix].append((name, node))\n\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 3: Force clustering into ~15 groups\n        target_groups = min(15, MAX_SUBCATEGORIES)\n        children_sorted = sorted(children, key=lambda x: x[0])\n        group_size = (num_children + target_groups - 1) // target_groups\n\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for i, (name, node) in enumerate(children_sorted):\n            group_idx = i // group_size\n            # Use first item's first word as group name\n            group_items = groups.get(group_idx, [])\n            if not group_items:\n                words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n                group_key = words[0] if words else f'Group{group_idx}'\n            else:\n                # Use existing key\n                existing_keys = [k for k in groups.keys() if isinstance(k, str)]\n                group_key = group_idx\n            groups[group_idx].append((name, node))\n\n        # Convert numeric keys to meaningful names\n        named_groups = {}\n        for key, members in groups.items():\n            first_name = members[0][0]\n            words = [w for w in first_name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            group_name = words[0] if words else f'Group{key}'\n            if group_name in named_groups:\n                group_name = f'{group_name}_{key}'\n            named_groups[group_name] = members\n\n        self._apply_grouping(parent, named_groups)\n\n    def _apply_grouping(self, parent, groups):\n        \"\"\"Apply grouping to parent node.\"\"\"\n        new_children = {}\n        for group_name, members in groups.items():\n            if len(members) == 1:\n                new_children[members[0][0]] = members[0][1]\n            else:\n                # Create grouped category name\n                member_names = [m[0] for m in members]\n                if isinstance(group_name, int):\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, member_names[0].split()[0])\n                else:\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, group_name)\n\n                # Avoid duplicate names\n                if group_cat_name in new_children:\n                    group_cat_name = f'{group_cat_name} | Group'\n\n                # Create intermediate node\n                intermediate = TaxonomyNode(group_cat_name, parent.level + 1)\n                for name, node in members:\n                    # Shorten child name by removing group words\n                    shortened = self._remove_parent_overlap(name, group_cat_name)\n                    if shortened and shortened != name:\n                        child_name = shortened\n                    else:\n                        child_name = name\n                    # Avoid duplicate child names\n                    if child_name in intermediate.children:\n                        child_name = f'{child_name} (Alt)'\n                    intermediate.children[child_name] = node\n                    self._update_levels(node, intermediate.level + 1)\n                    intermediate.sources.update(node.sources)\n\n                new_children[group_cat_name] = intermediate\n\n        parent.children = new_children\n\n    def _create_group_name(self, member_names, hint):\n        \"\"\"Create a representative name for a group.\"\"\"\n        word_counter = Counter()\n        for name in member_names:\n            words = get_word_set(name)\n            word_counter.update(words)\n\n        total = len(member_names)\n        representative = []\n\n        # First add the hint word\n        representative.append(hint.title())\n\n        # Add words appearing in 70%+ of members\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if word.lower() != hint.lower() and count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n            if len(representative) >= MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME:\n                break\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME])\n\n    def _update_records_for_grouping(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Update records after grouping (no-op for now, handled at output).\"\"\"\n        pass\n\n    def ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure siblings have less than 30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nChecking sibling distinctiveness...\")\n        self._check_sibling_overlap(self.root)\n\n    def _check_sibling_overlap(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Check and fix sibling overlap at a node.\"\"\"\n        siblings = list(node.children.keys())\n        renamed = {}\n\n        for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n            for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                if sib1 in renamed:\n                    sib1 = renamed[sib1]\n                if sib2 in renamed:\n                    sib2 = renamed[sib2]\n\n                overlap = calculate_word_overlap(sib1, sib2)\n                if overlap > MAX_SIBLING_OVERLAP:\n                    # Differentiate by adding unique words\n                    words1 = get_word_set(sib1)\n                    words2 = get_word_set(sib2)\n                    unique2 = words2 - words1\n\n                    if unique2:\n                        new_name = WORD_SEPARATOR.join([w.title() for w in list(unique2)[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME]])\n                        if new_name and new_name != sib2:\n                            self._rename_child(node, sib2, new_name)\n                            renamed[sib2] = new_name\n\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._check_sibling_overlap(child)\n\n    def _rename_child(self, parent, old_name, new_name):\n        \"\"\"Rename a child node.\"\"\"\n        if old_name in parent.children and new_name not in parent.children:\n            node = parent.children[old_name]\n            node.name = new_name\n            parent.children[new_name] = node\n            del parent.children[old_name]\n\n    def rebuild_records(self):\n        \"\"\"Rebuild all records to match current tree structure.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nRebuilding records to match tree...\")\n\n        # Build lookup from original path -> current tree path\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            current_path = self._find_path_in_tree(record['unified_levels'])\n            if current_path:\n                # Pad to 5 levels\n                while len(current_path) < MAX_LEVELS:\n                    current_path.append('')\n                record['unified_levels'] = current_path[:MAX_LEVELS]\n\n    def _find_path_in_tree(self, original_levels):\n        \"\"\"Find the current path in tree for original unified levels.\"\"\"\n        path = []\n        current = self.root\n\n        for level in original_levels:\n            if not level:\n                break\n\n            if level in current.children:\n                path.append(level)\n                current = current.children[level]\n            else:\n                # Try to find best match\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for child_name in current.children:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(level, child_name)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = child_name\n\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    path.append(best_match)\n                    current = current.children[best_match]\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        return path if path else [original_levels[0]] if original_levels and original_levels[0] else []\n\n    def check_source_distribution(self):\n        \"\"\"Check and report source distribution across taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nSource distribution in top-level categories:\")\n        for name, node in sorted(self.root.children.items()):\n            source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n            all_nodes = [node] + node.get_all_descendants()\n            for n in all_nodes:\n                for source in n.sources:\n                    source_counts[source] += 1\n\n            total = sum(source_counts.values())\n            if total > 0:\n                dist = {s: f\"{c/total:.1%}\" for s, c in sorted(source_counts.items())}\n                print(f\"  {name}: {dist}\")\n\n    def generate_full_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the full taxonomy DataFrame.\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            row = {\n                'source': record['source'],\n                'category_path': record['category_path'],\n                'depth': record['depth'],\n            }\n            for i, level in enumerate(record['unified_levels'], 1):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i}'] = level if level else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\n        # Ensure column order\n        cols = ['source', 'category_path', 'depth',\n                'unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2', 'unified_level_3',\n                'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5']\n        return df[cols]\n\n    def generate_hierarchy_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the hierarchy DataFrame with all unique paths.\"\"\"\n        paths = set()\n\n        def collect_paths(node, current_path):\n            if node.level > 0:\n                # Add all partial paths (from level 1 to current)\n                for i in range(len(current_path)):\n                    paths.add(tuple(current_path[:i+1]))\n\n            for child_name, child in node.children.items():\n                collect_paths(child, current_path + [child_name])\n\n        collect_paths(self.root, [])\n\n        # Convert to rows\n        rows = []\n        for path in sorted(paths):\n            row = {}\n            for i in range(MAX_LEVELS):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i+1}'] = path[i] if i < len(path) else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n        df = df.sort_values(\n            by=[f'unified_level_{i}' for i in range(1, MAX_LEVELS + 1)]\n        ).reset_index(drop=True)\n\n        return df\n\n    def print_statistics(self):\n        \"\"\"Print taxonomy statistics.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(\"UNIFIED TAXONOMY STATISTICS\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Top level count\n        print(f\"\\nTop-level categories: {len(self.root.children)}\")\n        for cat in sorted(self.root.children.keys()):\n            desc_count = len(self.root.children[cat].get_all_descendants())\n            print(f\"  - {cat}: {desc_count} descendants\")\n\n        # Source distribution\n        source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            source_counts[record['source']] += 1\n        print(f\"\\nRecords by source:\")\n        for source, count in sorted(source_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  {source}: {count}\")\n\n        # Depth distribution\n        depth_counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            depth_counts[record['depth']] += 1\n        print(f\"\\nRecords by depth:\")\n        for depth, count in sorted(depth_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  Level {depth}: {count}\")\n\n        # Children per level\n        def count_at_level(node, target_level, current_level=0):\n            if current_level == target_level:\n                return [len(node.children)] if node.children else []\n            counts = []\n            for child in node.children.values():\n                counts.extend(count_at_level(child, target_level, current_level + 1))\n            return counts\n\n        print(f\"\\nChildren distribution:\")\n        for level in range(1, MAX_LEVELS):\n            counts = count_at_level(self.root, level)\n            counts = [c for c in counts if c > 0]\n            if counts:\n                avg = sum(counts) / len(counts)\n                min_c, max_c = min(counts), max(counts)\n                print(f\"  Level {level}: avg {avg:.1f} children (range: {min_c}-{max_c})\")\n\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading category data...\")\n\n    # Load all data\n    amazon_df = load_amazon_data()\n    facebook_df = load_facebook_data()\n    google_df = load_google_data()\n\n    print(f\"  Amazon: {len(amazon_df)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Facebook: {len(facebook_df)} categories\")\n    print(f\"  Google: {len(google_df)} categories\")\n\n    # Initialize builder\n    builder = UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder()\n\n    # Process Amazon\n    print(\"\\nProcessing Amazon categories...\")\n    for _, row in amazon_df.iterrows():\n        levels = parse_category_path(row['category_path'])\n        if levels:\n            builder.add_category('amazon', row['category_path'], levels)\n\n    # Process Facebook\n    print(\"Processing Facebook categories...\")\n    for _, row in facebook_df.iterrows():\n        levels = STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES and cat != 'Other':\n                # Find best match from standard categories\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for std_cat in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES:\n                    if std_cat in self.root.children:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, std_cat)\n                        if sim > best_sim:\n                            best_sim = sim\n                            best_match = std_cat\n\n                # If good match found, merge\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    self._merge_top_level(cat, best_match)\n                elif 'Other' in current_cats or cat == 'Other':\n                    pass  # Keep as is\n                else:\n                    # Find any similar category\n                    for other_cat in list(self.root.children.keys()):\n                        if other_cat != cat:\n                            sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, other_cat)\n                            if sim > 0.4:\n                                # Merge into the larger one\n                                size_cat = len(self.root.children[cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                size_other = len(self.root.children[other_cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                if size_cat > calculate_word_overlap(smallest, other)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = other\n\n            if best_match:\n                self._merge_top_level(smallest, best_match)\n            else:\n                # Merge with largest for balance\n                del self.root.children[smallest]\n\n        print(f\"After: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n    def _merge_top_level(self, source_name, target_name):\n        \"\"\"Merge source top-level category into target.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in self.root.children or target_name not in self.root.children:\n            return\n        if source_name == target_name:\n            return\n\n        source = self.root.children[source_name]\n        target = self.root.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge all children\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                self._update_levels(child_node, target.level + 1)\n\n        # Merge metadata\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n\n        # Update all records that used source_name\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            if record['unified_levels'][0] == source_name:\n                record['unified_levels'][0] = target_name\n\n        # Remove source\n        del self.root.children[source_name]\n        print(f\"  Merged '{source_name}' into '{target_name}'\")\n\n    def _merge_subtrees(self, target, source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _update_levels(self, node, new_level):\n        \"\"\"Update level of node and all descendants.\"\"\"\n        node.level = new_level\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._update_levels(child, new_level + 1)\n\n    def balance_subtrees(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance all subtrees to meet 3-20 children constraint.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBalancing subtrees...\")\n        self._balance_node(self.root, max_depth=MAX_LEVELS)\n\n    def _balance_node(self, node, max_depth):\n        \"\"\"Recursively balance a node's children.\"\"\"\n        if node.level >= max_depth:\n            return\n\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if num_children > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # Recurse to children\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_node(child, max_depth)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children when there are too many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n        num_children = len(children)\n\n        # Strategy 1: Group by first len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 2: Group by common word prefix (first 2 words)\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            prefix = ' '.join(words[:2]) if self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 3: Force clustering into ~15 groups\n        target_groups = min(15, MAX_SUBCATEGORIES)\n        children_sorted = _apply_grouping(self, parent, groups):\n        \"\"\"Apply grouping to parent node.\"\"\"\n        new_children = {}\n        for group_name, members in groups.items():\n            if len(members) word\n        representative.append(hint.title())\n\n        # Add words appearing in 70%+ of members\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if word.lower() != hint.lower() and count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n            if len(representative) >= MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME:\n                break\n\n return set(w for w in words if w not in stop_words and len(w) > 1)\n\ndef calculate_word_overlap(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage between two texts.\"\"\"\n    words1 = return len(intersection) / min_size if min_size > 0 else 0.0\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TOP-LEVEL CATEGORY MAPPING\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Define 15 standard top-level categories (target within 10-20 range)\nSTANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES = [\n    'Electronics | Technology',\n    'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'Home | Garden',\n    'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'Toys | Games',\n    'Food | Beverages',\n    'Health | Household',\n    'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'Books | Media',\n    'Office | Supplies',\n    'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'Pet Supplies',\n    'Baby | Kids',\n    'Arts | Crafts',\n]\n\n# Categories to merge or rename (source -> target)\nMERGE_CATEGORIES = {\n    'Other': 'Home | Garden',\n    'Religious & Ceremonial': 'Arts | Crafts',\n}\n\n# Rename categories to match standard naming\nRENAME_CATEGORIES = {\n    'Office': 'Office | Supplies',\n}\n\n# Map source category names to standard top-level names\nTOP_LEVEL_MAPPING = {\n    # Electronics\n    'electronics': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'consumer electronics': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'video games': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'computers': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'camera & photo': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'cell phones & accessories': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'cameras & optics': 'Electronics | Technology',\n    'software': 'Electronics | Technology',\n\n    # Clothing\n    'clothing': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'clothing, shoes & jewelry': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'fashion': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'apparel & accessories': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"women's fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"men's fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"girls' fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    \"boys' fashion\": 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'clothing & accessories': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'jewelry & watches': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n\n    # Home & Garden\n    'home & kitchen': 'Home | Garden',\n    'home & garden': 'Home | Garden',\n    'furniture': 'Home | Garden',\n    'garden & outdoor': 'Home | Garden',\n    'patio, lawn & garden': 'Home | Garden',\n    'tools & home improvement': 'Home | Garden',\n    'kitchen & dining': 'Home | Garden',\n    'home': 'Home | Garden',\n    'home improvement': 'Home | Garden',\n    'patio & garden': 'Home | Garden',\n    'appliances': 'Home | Garden',\n\n    # Health & Beauty\n    'health & household': 'Health | Household',\n    'health & beauty': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'beauty & personal care': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'beauty': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'personal care': 'Beauty | Personal Care',\n\n    # Food & Beverages\n    'grocery & gourmet food': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'food & beverages': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'food': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'beverages': 'Food | Beverages',\n    'tobacco': 'Food | Beverages',\n\n    # Sports & Outdoors\n    'sports & outdoors': 'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'sporting goods': 'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'outdoor recreation': 'Sports | Outdoors',\n\n    # Books & Media\n    'books': 'Books | Media',\n    'movies & tv': 'Books | Media',\n    'cds & vinyl': 'Books | Media',\n    'digital music': 'Books | Media',\n    'media': 'Books | Media',\n    'arts & entertainment': 'Books | Media',\n    'antiques & collectibles': 'Books | Media',\n\n    # Pet\n    'pet supplies': 'Pet Supplies',\n    'animals & pet supplies': 'Pet Supplies',\n\n    # Baby\n    'baby': 'Baby | Kids',\n    'baby products': 'Baby | Kids',\n    'baby & toddler': 'Baby | Kids',\n\n    # Office\n    'office products': 'Office | Supplies',\n    'office supplies': 'Office | Supplies',\n    'office': 'Office | Supplies',\n\n    # Industrial\n    'industrial & scientific': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'business & industrial': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'hardware': 'Industrial | Scientific',\n\n    # Arts\n    'arts, crafts & sewing': 'Arts | Crafts',\n    'arts & crafts': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Automotive\n    'automotive': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'vehicles & parts': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'auto parts & accessories': 'Automotive | Vehicles',\n\n    # Toys\n    'toys & games': 'Toys | Games',\n    'toys': 'Toys | Games',\n    'games': 'Toys | Games',\n\n    # Travel/Luggage -> map to Clothing\n    'luggage & travel gear': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'luggage & bags': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'travel & luggage': 'Clothing | Fashion',\n\n    # Musical Instruments -> Arts\n    'musical instruments': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Mature\n    'mature': 'Health | Household',\n\n    # Religious -> Arts\n    'religious items & ceremonial': 'Arts | Crafts',\n\n    # Other\n    'other': 'Other',\n}\n\ndef map_to_top_level(category_name):\n    \"\"\"Map a category name to a standardized top-level category.\"\"\"\n    if not category_name:\n        return 'Other'\n    key = category_name.lower().strip()\n    return TOP_LEVEL_MAPPING.get(key, None)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TAXONOMY NODE\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass TaxonomyNode:\n    \"\"\"Represents a node in the taxonomy hierarchy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, name, level=0):\n        self.name = name\n        self.level = level\n        self.children = {}\n        self.sources = set()\n        self.original_paths = []\n        self.record_refs = []  # References to records for updating\n\n    def add_child(self, name):\n        \"\"\"Add or get a child node.\"\"\"\n        if name not in self.children:\n            self.children[name] = TaxonomyNode(name, self.level + 1)\n        return self.children[name]\n\n    def get_child_count(self):\n        \"\"\"Get number of children.\"\"\"\n        return len(self.children)\n\n    def get_all_descendants(self):\n        \"\"\"Get all descendant nodes.\"\"\"\n        descendants = list(self.children.values())\n        for child in list(self.children.values()):\n            descendants.extend(child.get_all_descendants())\n        return descendants\n\n# ============================================================================\n# UNIFIED TAXONOMY BUILDER\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass UnifiedTaxonomyBuilder:\n    \"\"\"Builds and manages the unified taxonomy.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.root = TaxonomyNode('ROOT', 0)\n        self.all_records = []\n\n    def add_category(self, source, original_path, levels):\n        \"\"\"Add a category path to the taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        if not levels:\n            return\n\n        # Map first level to standardized top-level category\n        top_level = map_to_top_level(levels[0])\n\n        if top_level is None:\n            # Try partial match\n            key = levels[0].lower().strip()\n            for pattern, mapped in TOP_LEVEL_MAPPING.items():\n                if pattern in key or key in pattern:\n                    top_level = mapped\n                    break\n\n            if top_level is None:\n                # Use normalized original as fallback\n                top_level = normalize_text(levels[0])\n\n        unified_levels = [top_level]\n\n        # Process remaining levels (levels 2-5)\n        for i, level in enumerate(levels[1:MAX_LEVELS], 1):\n            normalized = normalize_text(level)\n            # Remove parent word overlap to avoid redundancy\n            normalized = self._remove_parent_overlap(normalized, unified_levels[-1])\n            if normalized:\n                unified_levels.append(normalized)\n            else:\n                unified_levels.append(normalize_text(level))\n\n        # Pad to 5 levels\n        while len(unified_levels) < MAX_LEVELS:\n            unified_levels.append('')\n\n        # Only keep first 5 levels\n        unified_levels = unified_levels[:MAX_LEVELS]\n\n        # Add to tree\n        current = self.root\n        for i, level_name in enumerate(unified_levels):\n            if not level_name:\n                break\n            current = current.add_child(level_name)\n            current.sources.add(source)\n\n        # Store record\n        record = {\n            'source': source,\n            'category_path': original_path,\n            'depth': min(len([l for l in levels if l]), MAX_LEVELS),\n            'unified_levels': unified_levels.copy()\n        }\n        self.all_records.append(record)\n\n    def _remove_parent_overlap(self, child_name, parent_name):\n        \"\"\"Remove words from child that appear in parent.\"\"\"\n        parent_words = get_word_set(parent_name)\n        child_words = child_name.split()\n        filtered = [w for w in child_words if w.lower() not in parent_words]\n        if not filtered:\n            return child_name\n        return ' '.join(filtered)\n\n    def consolidate_top_level(self):\n        \"\"\"Consolidate top-level categories.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nConsolidating top-level categories...\")\n        print(f\"Before: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n        # First, apply explicit renames\n        for old_name, new_name in RENAME_CATEGORIES.items():\n            if old_name in self.root.children and new_name not in self.root.children:\n                node = self.root.children[old_name]\n                node.name = new_name\n                self.root.children[new_name] = node\n                del self.root.children[old_name]\n                # Update records\n                for record in self.all_records:\n                    if record['unified_levels'][0] == old_name:\n                        record['unified_levels'][0] = new_name\n                print(f\"  Renamed '{old_name}' to '{new_name}'\")\n\n        # Apply explicit merges from MERGE_CATEGORIES\n        for source_cat, target_cat in MERGE_CATEGORIES.items():\n            if source_cat in self.root.children:\n                if target_cat not in self.root.children:\n                    # Create target if it doesn't exist\n                    self.root.children[target_cat] = TaxonomyNode(target_cat, 1)\n                self._merge_top_level(source_cat, target_cat)\n\n        # Find categories that should be merged\n        current_cats = list(self.root.children.keys())\n\n        # Merge any unmapped categories into closest standard category\n        for cat in current_cats:\n            if cat not in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES and cat != 'Other':\n                # Find best match from standard categories\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for std_cat in STANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES:\n                    if std_cat in self.root.children:\n                        sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, std_cat)\n                        if sim > best_sim:\n                            best_sim = sim\n                            best_match = std_cat\n\n                # If good match found, merge\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    self._merge_top_level(cat, best_match)\n                elif 'Other' in current_cats or cat == 'Other':\n                    pass  # Keep as is\n                else:\n                    # Find any similar category\n                    for other_cat in list(self.root.children.keys()):\n                        if other_cat != cat:\n                            sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, other_cat)\n                            if sim > 0.4:\n                                # Merge into the larger one\n                                size_cat = len(self.root.children[cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                size_other = len(self.root.children[other_cat].get_all_descendants())\n                                if size_cat > size_other:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(other_cat, cat)\n                                else:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(cat, other_cat)\n                                break\n\n        # If still too many, merge smallest\n        while len(self.root.children) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n            sizes = {k: len(v.get_all_descendants()) + 1\n                    for k, v in self.root.children.items()}\n            smallest = min(sizes, key=sizes.get)\n\n            # Find most similar category to merge with\n            best_match = None\n            best_sim = 0\n            for other in self.root.children:\n                if other != smallest:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(smallest, other)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = other\n\n            if best_match:\n                self._merge_top_level(smallest, best_match)\n            else:\n                # Merge with largest for balance\n                del self.root.children[smallest]\n\n        print(f\"After: {len(self.root.children)} categories\")\n\n    def _merge_top_level(self, source_name, target_name):\n        \"\"\"Merge source top-level category into target.\"\"\"\n        if source_name not in self.root.children or target_name not in self.root.children:\n            return\n        if source_name == target_name:\n            return\n\n        source = self.root.children[source_name]\n        target = self.root.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge all children\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                self._update_levels(child_node, target.level + 1)\n\n        # Merge metadata\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n\n        # Update all records that used source_name\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            if record['unified_levels'][0] == source_name:\n                record['unified_levels'][0] = target_name\n\n        # Remove source\n        del self.root.children[source_name]\n        print(f\"  Merged '{source_name}' into '{target_name}'\")\n\n    def _merge_subtrees(self, target, source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _update_levels(self, node, new_level):\n        \"\"\"Update level of node and all descendants.\"\"\"\n        node.level = new_level\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._update_levels(child, new_level + 1)\n\n    def balance_subtrees(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance all subtrees to meet 3-20 children constraint.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBalancing subtrees...\")\n        self._balance_node(self.root, max_depth=MAX_LEVELS)\n\n    def _balance_node(self, node, max_depth):\n        \"\"\"Recursively balance a node's children.\"\"\"\n        if node.level >= max_depth:\n            return\n\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if num_children > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # Recurse to children\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_node(child, max_depth)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children when there are too many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n        num_children = len(children)\n\n        # Strategy 1: Group by first significant word\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            first_word = words[0] if words else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping by first word helps enough\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 2: Group by common word prefix (first 2 words)\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            prefix = ' '.join(words[:2]) if len(words) >= 2 else (words[0] if words else 'Other')\n            groups[prefix].append((name, node))\n\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 3: Force clustering into ~15 groups\n        target_groups = min(15, MAX_SUBCATEGORIES)\n        children_sorted = sorted(children, key=lambda x: x[0])\n        group_size = (num_children + target_groups - 1) // target_groups\n\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for i, (name, node) in enumerate(children_sorted):\n            group_idx = i // group_size\n            # Use first item's first word as group name\n            group_items = groups.get(group_idx, [])\n            if not group_items:\n                words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n                group_key = words[0] if words else f'Group{group_idx}'\n            else:\n                # Use existing key\n                existing_keys = [k for k in groups.keys() if isinstance(k, str)]\n                group_key = group_idx\n            groups[group_idx].append((name, node))\n\n        # Convert numeric keys to meaningful names\n        named_groups = {}\n        for key, members in groups.items():\n            first_name = members[0][0]\n            words = [w for w in first_name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            group_name = words[0] if words else f'Group{key}'\n            if group_name in named_groups:\n                group_name = f'{group_name}_{key}'\n            named_groups[group_name] = members\n\n        self._apply_grouping(parent, named_groups)\n\n    def _apply_grouping(self, parent, groups):\n        \"\"\"Apply grouping to parent node.\"\"\"\n        new_children = {}\n        for group_name, members in groups.items():\n            if len(members) == 1:\n                new_children[members[0][0]] = members[0][1]\n            else:\n                # Create grouped category name\n                member_names = [m[0] for m in members]\n                if isinstance(group_name, int):\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, member_names[0].split()[0])\n                else:\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, group_name)\n\n                # Avoid duplicate names\n                if group_cat_name in new_children:\n                    group_cat_name = f'{group_cat_name} | Group'\n\n                # Create intermediate node\n                intermediate = TaxonomyNode(group_cat_name, parent.level + 1)\n                for name, node in members:\n                    # Shorten child name by removing group words\n                    shortened = self._remove_parent_overlap(name, group_cat_name)\n                    if shortened and shortened != name:\n                        child_name = shortened\n                    else:\n                        child_name = name\n                    # Avoid duplicate child names\n                    if child_name in intermediate.children:\n                        child_name = f'{child_name} (Alt)'\n                    intermediate.children[child_name] = node\n                    self._update_levels(node, intermediate.level + 1)\n                    intermediate.sources.update(node.sources)\n\n                new_children[group_cat_name] = intermediate\n\n        parent.children = new_children\n\n    def _create_group_name(self, member_names, hint):\n        \"\"\"Create a representative name for a group.\"\"\"\n        word_counter = Counter()\n        for name in member_names:\n            words = get_word_set(name)\n            word_counter.update(words)\n\n        total = len(member_names)\n        representative = []\n\n        # First add the hint word\n        representative.append(hint.title())\n\n        # Add words appearing in 70%+ of members\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if word.lower() != hint.lower() and count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n            if len(representative) >= MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME:\n                break\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME])\n\n    def _update_records_for_grouping(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Update records after grouping (no-op for now, handled at output).\"\"\"\n        pass\n\n    def ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure siblings have less than 30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nChecking sibling distinctiveness...\")\n        self._check_sibling_overlap(self.root)\n\n    def _check_sibling_overlap(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Check and fix sibling overlap at a node.\"\"\"\n        siblings = list(node.children.keys())\n        renamed = {}\n\n        for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n            for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                if sib1 in renamed:\n                    sib1 = renamed[sib1]\n                if sib2 in renamed:\n                    sib2 = renamed[sib2]\n\n                overlap = calculate_word_overlap(sib1, sib2)\n                if overlap > MAX_SIBLING_OVERLAP:\n                    # Differentiate by adding unique words\n                    words1 = get_word_set(sib1)\n                    words2 = get_word_set(sib2)\n                    unique2 = words2 - words1\n\n                    if unique2:\n                        new_name = WORD_SEPARATOR.join([w.title() for w in list(unique2)[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME]])\n                        if new_name and new_name != sib2:\n                            self._rename_child(node, sib2, new_name)\n                            renamed[sib2] = new_name\n\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._check_sibling_overlap(child)\n\n    def _rename_child(self, parent, old_name, new_name):\n        \"\"\"Rename a child node.\"\"\"\n        if old_name in parent.children and new_name not in parent.children:\n            node = parent.children[old_name]\n            node.name = new_name\n            parent.children[new_name] = node\n            del parent.children[old_name]\n\n    def rebuild_records(self):\n        \"\"\"Rebuild all records to match current tree structure.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nRebuilding records to match tree...\")\n\n        # Build lookup from original path -> current tree path\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            current_path = self._find_path_in_tree(record['unified_levels'])\n            if current_path:\n                # Pad to 5 levels\n                while len(current_path) < MAX_LEVELS:\n                    current_path.append('')\n                record['unified_levels'] = current_path[:MAX_LEVELS]\n\n    def _find_path_in_tree(self, original_levels):\n        \"\"\"Find the current path in tree for original unified levels.\"\"\"\n        path = []\n        current = self.root\n\n        for level in original_levels:\n            if not level:\n                break\n\n            if level in current.children:\n                path.append(level)\n                current = current.children[level]\n            else:\n                # Try to find best match\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for child_name in current.children:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(level, child_name)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = child_name\n\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    path.append(best_match)\n                    current = current.children[best_match]\n                else:\n                    break\n\n        return path if path else [original_levels[0]] if original_levels and original_levels[0] else []\n\n    def check_source_distribution(self):\n        \"\"\"Check and report source distribution across taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nSource distribution in top-level categories:\")\n        for name, node in sorted(self.root.children.items()):\n            source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n            all_nodes = [node] + node.get_all_descendants()\n            for n in all_nodes:\n                for source in n.sources:\n                    source_counts[source] += 1\n\n            total = sum(source_counts.values())\n            if total > 0:\n                dist = {s: f\"{c/total:.1%}\" for s, c in sorted(source_counts.items())}\n                print(f\"  {name}: {dist}\")\n\n    def generate_full_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the full taxonomy DataFrame.\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            row = {\n                'source': record['source'],\n                'category_path': record['category_path'],\n                'depth': record['depth'],\n            }\n            for i, level in enumerate(record['unified_levels'], 1):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i}'] = level if level else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\n        # Ensure column order\n        cols = ['source', 'category_path', 'depth',\n                'unified_level_1', 'unified_level_2', 'unified_level_3',\n                'unified_level_4', 'unified_level_5']\n        return df[cols]\n\n    def generate_hierarchy_output(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the hierarchy DataFrame with all unique paths.\"\"\"\n        paths = set()\n\n        def collect_paths(node, current_path):\n            if node.level > 0:\n                # Add all partial paths (from level 1 to current)\n                for i in range(len(current_path)):\n                    paths.add(tuple(current_path[:i+1]))\n\n            for child_name, child in node.children.items():\n                collect_paths(child, current_path + [child_name])\n\n        collect_paths(self.root, [])\n\n        # Convert to rows\n        rows = []\n        for path in sorted(paths):\n            row = {}\n            for i in range(MAX_LEVELS):\n                row[f'unified_level_{i+1}'] = path[i] if i < len(path) else ''\n            rows.append(row)\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n        df = df.sort_values(\n            by=[f'unified_level_{i}' for i in range(1, MAX_LEVELS + 1)]\n        ).reset_index(drop=True)\n\n        return df\n\n    def print_statistics(self):\n        \"\"\"Print taxonomy statistics.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(\"UNIFIED TAXONOMY STATISTICS\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Top level count\n        print(f\"\\nTop-level categories: {len(self.root.children)}\")\n        for cat in sorted(self.root.children.keys()):\n            desc_count = len(self.root.children[cat].get_all_descendants())\n            print(f\"  - {cat}: {desc_count} descendants\")\n\n        # Source distribution\n        source_counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for record in self.all_records:\n            source_counts[record['source']] += 1\n        print(f\"\\nRecords by source:\")\n 'unified_levels': unified_levels.copy()\n        }\n        self.all_records.append(record)\n\n    def _remove_parent_overlap(self, child_name, parent_name):\n        \"\"\"Remove words from child        if not filtered:\n            return child_name\n        return ' '.join(filtered)\n\n    def consolidate_top_level(self):\n        \"\"\"Consolidate top-level categories.\"\"\"\n                 if other_cat != cat:\n                            sim = calculate_word_overlap(cat, other_cat)\n                            if sim > 0.4:\n                                # Merge into                        else:\n                                    self._merge_top_level(cat, other_cat)\n                                break\n\n        # If still too many, merge smallest\n        while len(self.root.children) > MAX_TOP_LEVEL:\n            sizes = {k: len(v.get_all_descendants()) + 1\n                    for k, v in self.root.children.items()}\n            smallest = min(sizes, key=sizes.get)\n\n            # Find most similar category to merge with\n            best_match = None\n            best_sim = 0\n            for other in self.root.children:\n                if best_match = other\n\n            if best_match:\n                self._merge_top_level(smallest, best_match)\n            else:\n                # Merge with largest for balance\n                del return\n\n        source = self.root.children[source_name]\n        target = self.root.children[target_name]\n\n        # Merge all children\n        for child_name, child_node in target.children[child_name] = child_node\n                self._update_levels(child_node, target.level + 1)\n\n        # Merge metadata\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n\n        # source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n            if child_name in target.children:\n                self._merge_subtrees(target.children[child_name], child_node)\n            else:\n                target.children[child_name] = child_node\n\n    def _update_levels(self, node, new_level):\n        \"\"\"Update level of node and all descendants.\"\"\"\n        node.level = new_level\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._update_levels(child, new_level + 1)\n\n    def balance_subtrees(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance all subtrees to meet 3-20 children constraint.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBalancing subtrees...\")\n        self._balance_node(self.root, max_depth=MAX_LEVELS)\n\n    def _balance_node(self, node, max_depth):\n        \"\"\"Recursively balance a node's children.\"\"\"\n        if node.level >= max_depth:\n            return\n\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if num_children > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # Recurse to children\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_node(child, max_depth)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children when there are too many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n        num_children = len(children)\n\n        # Strategy 1: Group by first significant word\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            first_word = words[0] if words else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping by first word helps enough\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 2: Group by common word prefix (first 2 words)\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            prefix = ' '.join(words[:2]) if len(words) >= 2 else (words[0] if words else 'Other')\n            groups[prefix].append((name, node))\n\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 3: Force clustering into ~15 groups\n        target_groups = min(15, MAX_SUBCATEGORIES)\n enumerate(children_sorted):\n            group_idx = i // group_size\n            # Use first item's first word as group name\n            group_items = groups.get(group_idx, [])\n            if not source):\n        \"\"\"Recursively merge source subtree into target.\"\"\"\n        target.sources.update(source.sources)\n        for child_name, child_node in source.children.items():\n _update_levels(self, node, new_level):\n        \"\"\"Update level of node and all descendants.\"\"\"\n        node.level = new_level\n        for child in node.children.values():\n            self._update_levels(child, new_level + 1)\n\n    def balance_subtrees(self):\n        \"\"\"Balance all subtrees to meet 3-20 children constraint.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBalancing subtrees...\")\n        self._balance_node(self.root, max_depth=MAX_LEVELS)\n\n    def _balance_node(self, node, max_depth):\n        \"\"\"Recursively balance a node's children.\"\"\"\n        if node.level >= max_depth:\n            return\n\n        num_children = len(node.children)\n\n        if num_children > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            self._cluster_children(node)\n\n        # Recurse to children\n        for child in list(node.children.values()):\n            self._balance_node(child, max_depth)\n\n    def _cluster_children(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Cluster children when there are too many.\"\"\"\n        if len(parent.children) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES:\n            return\n\n        children = list(parent.children.items())\n        num_children = len(children)\n\n        # Strategy 1: Group by first significant word\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            first_word = words[0] if words else 'Other'\n            groups[first_word].append((name, node))\n\n        # If grouping by first word helps enough\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 2: Group by common word prefix (first 2 words)\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for name, node in children:\n            words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            prefix = ' '.join(words[:2]) if len(words) >= 2 else (words[0] if words else 'Other')\n            groups[prefix].append((name, node))\n\n        if len(groups) <= MAX_SUBCATEGORIES and len(groups) < num_children:\n            self._apply_grouping(parent, groups)\n            return\n\n        # Strategy 3: Force clustering into ~15 groups\n        target_groups = min(15, MAX_SUBCATEGORIES)\n        children_sorted = sorted(children, key=lambda x: x[0])\n        group_size = (num_children + target_groups - 1) // target_groups\n\n        groups = defaultdict(list)\n        for i, (name, node) in enumerate(children_sorted):\n            group_idx = i // group_size\n            # Use first item's first word as group name\n            group_items = groups.get(group_idx, [])\n            if not group_items:\n                words = [w for w in name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n                group_key = words[0] if words else f'Group{group_idx}'\n            else:\n                # Use existing key\n                existing_keys = [k for k in groups.keys() if isinstance(k, str)]\n                group_key = group_idx\n            groups[group_idx].append((name, node))\n\n        # Convert numeric keys to meaningful names\n        named_groups = {}\n        for key, members in groups.items():\n            first_name = members[0][0]\n            words = [w for w in first_name.split() if w.lower() not in {'and', 'or', '&', '|', 'the'}]\n            group_name = words[0] if words else f'Group{key}'\n            if group_name in named_groups:\n                group_name = f'{group_name}_{key}'\n            named_groups[group_name] = members\n\n        self._apply_grouping(parent, named_groups)\n\n    def _apply_grouping(self, parent, groups):\n        \"\"\"Apply grouping to parent node.\"\"\"\n        new_children = {}\n        for group_name, members in groups.items():\n            if len(members) == 1:\n                new_children[members[0][0]] = members[0][1]\n            else:\n                # Create grouped category name\n                member_names = [m[0] for m in members]\n                if isinstance(group_name, int):\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, member_names[0].split()[0])\n                else:\n                    group_cat_name = self._create_group_name(member_names, group_name)\n\n                # Avoid duplicate names\n                if group_cat_name in new_children:\n                    group_cat_name = f'{group_cat_name} | Group'\n\n                # Create intermediate node\n                intermediate = TaxonomyNode(group_cat_name, parent.level + 1)\n                for name, node in members:\n                    # Shorten child name by removing group words\n                    shortened = self._remove_parent_overlap(name, group_cat_name)\n                    if shortened and shortened != name:\n                        child_name = shortened\n                    else:\n                        child_name = name\n                    # Avoid duplicate child names\n                    if child_name in intermediate.children:\n                        child_name = f'{child_name} (Alt)'\n                    intermediate.children[child_name] = node\n                    self._update_levels(node, intermediate.level + 1)\n                    intermediate.sources.update(node.sources)\n\n                new_children[group_cat_name] = intermediate\n\n        parent.children = new_children\n\n    def _create_group_name(self, member_names, hint):\n        \"\"\"Create a representative name for a group.\"\"\"\n        word_counter = Counter()\n        for name in member_names:\n            words = get_word_set(name)\n            word_counter.update(words)\n\n        total = len(member_names)\n        representative = []\n\n        # First add the hint word\n        representative.append(hint.title())\n\n        # Add words appearing in 70%+ of members\n        for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n            if word.lower() != hint.lower() and count / total >= REPRESENTATIVE_THRESHOLD:\n                representative.append(word.title())\n            if len(representative) >= MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME:\n                break\n\n        return WORD_SEPARATOR.join(representative[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME])\n\n    def _update_records_for_grouping(self, parent):\n        \"\"\"Update records after grouping (no-op for now, handled at output).\"\"\"\n        pass\n\n    def ensure_sibling_distinctiveness(self):\n        \"\"\"Ensure siblings have less than 30% word overlap.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nChecking sibling distinctiveness...\")\n        self._check_sibling_overlap(self.root)\n\n    def _check_sibling_overlap(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Check and fix sibling overlap at a node.\"\"\"\n        siblings = list(node.children.keys())\n        renamed = {}\n\n        for i, sib1 in enumerate(siblings):\n            for sib2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n                if sib1 in renamed:\n                    sib1 = renamed[sib1]\n                if sib2 in renamed:\n                    sib2 = renamed[sib2]\n\n                overlap = calculate_word_overlap(sib1, sib2)\n                if overlap > MAX_SIBLING_OVERLAP:\n                    # Differentiate by adding unique words\n                    words1 = get_word_set(sib1)\n                    words2 = get_word_set(sib2)\n                    unique2 = words2 - words1\n\n                    if unique2:\n                        new_name = WORD_SEPARATOR.join([w.title() for w in list(unique2)[:MAX_WORDS_IN_NAME]])\n                        if new_name and new_name != sib2:\n                            self._rename_child(node, sib2, new_name)\n                            renamed[sib2] = new_name\n\n        for child in parent.children and new_name not in parent.children:\n            node = parent.children[old_name]\n            node.name = new_name\n            parent.children[new_name] = node\n            del current_path:\n                # Pad to 5 levels\n                while len(current_path) < MAX_LEVELS:\n                    current_path.append('')\n                record['unified_levels'] = self.root\n\n        for level in original_levels:\n            if not level:\n                break\n\n            if level in current.children:\n                path.append(level)\n                current = current.children[level]\n            else:\n                # Try to find best match\n                best_match = None\n                best_sim = 0\n                for child_name in current.children:\n                    sim = calculate_word_overlap(level, child_name)\n                    if sim > best_sim:\n                        best_sim = sim\n                        best_match = child_name\n\n                if best_match and best_sim > 0.3:\n                    path.append(best_match)\n                    current = current.children[best_match]\n \"\"\"Check and report source distribution across taxonomy.\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nSource distribution in top-level categories:\")\n        for name, node in sorted(self.root.children.items()):\n Title case\n    text = text.title()\n    # Clean extra whitespace\n    text = ' '.join(text.split())\n    return text\n\ndef get_word_set(text):\n    \"\"\"Get set of meaningful words from text.\"\"\"\n    stop_words = {'and', 'or', 'the', '&', 'for', 'of', 'in', 'with', 'to', 'a', 'an'}\n    words = re.findall(r'\\b\\w+\\b', text.lower())\n    return set(w for w in words if w not in stop_words and len(w) > 1)\n\ndef calculate_word_overlap(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap percentage between two texts.\"\"\"\n    words1 = get_word_set(text1)\n    words2 = get_word_set(text2)\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    min_size = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n    return len(intersection) / min_size if min_size > 0 else 0.0\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TOP-LEVEL CATEGORY MAPPING\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Define 15 standard top-level categories (target within 10-20 range)\nSTANDARD_TOP_CATEGORIES = [\n    'Electronics | Technology',\n    'Clothing | Fashion',\n    'Home | Garden',\n    'Beauty | Personal Care',\n    'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'Toys | Games',\n    'Food | Beverages',\n    'Health | Household',\n    'Automotive | Vehicles',\n    'Books | Media',\n    'Office | Supplies',\n    'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'Pet Supplies',\n    'Baby | Kids',\n    'Arts | Crafts',\n]\n\n# Categories to merge or rename (source -> target)\nMERGE_CATEGORIES = {\n    'Other': 'Home | Garden',\n    'Religious & Ceremonial': 'Arts | Crafts',\n}\n\n# Rename categories to match standard naming (applied after initial mapping)\nRENAME_CATEGORIES = {}\n\n# Map source category names to standard top-level names\nTOP_LEVEL_MAPPING = {\n    #
